# API reference

Generated by `tools/generate_api_reference.py` from each package's `__all__`; 3621 public names in 35 packages. Each entry shows the first line of the docstring; the full docstring states arguments, equations (`EQ-*`, see [equation-registry.md](equation-registry.md)) and references (`REF-*`, see [references.md](references.md)). Each package heading carries its stability label (ADR-0048): *stable* changes only after a deprecation period, *provisional* may still change but is deprecated first where practical, *experimental* may change in any release.

- [Foundations](#foundations)
- [Probability and statistics](#probability-and-statistics)
- [Inference and modelling](#inference-and-modelling)
- [Forward uncertainty quantification](#forward-uncertainty-quantification)
- [Backward analysis and validation](#backward-analysis-and-validation)
- [Reliability and risk](#reliability-and-risk)
- [Cost analysis](#cost-analysis)
- [Aerospace engineering](#aerospace-engineering)
- [Method development](#method-development)
- [Orchestration and figures](#orchestration-and-figures)

## Foundations

Seeds and validation, special functions, dense linear algebra, optimizers, and sampling designs.

### `probstat.foundations` · stable

Dependency-free validation and numerical foundation utilities.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `AskTellSession` | class | Drive a procedure by hand: ``ask`` for points, ``tell`` the outputs. |
| `batch_from_pointwise` | function | Wrap a function of one point as a batch model. |
| `BatchModel` | constant |  |
| `coerce_finite_sample` | function | Materialize a non-empty iterable of finite real observations. |
| `ContinuousProbabilityLaw` | class | A continuous univariate law with PDF, CDF, mean, and variance. |
| `CumulativeDistribution` | class | A univariate law that can evaluate a cumulative distribution function. |
| `deprecated` | function | Return a decorator that marks a callable as deprecated. |
| `derive_seed` | function | Derive a reproducible child seed from a root seed and a key path. |
| `DiscreteProbabilityLaw` | class | A discrete univariate law with PMF, CDF, mean, and variance. |
| `EvaluationRequest` | class | A set of input points to be evaluated by one named model. |
| `HasDimension` | class | A vector-valued law on a space of fixed dimension ``d``. |
| `HasMeanAndVariance` | class | A probability law exposing finite mean and variance summaries. |
| `HazardFunction` | class | A continuous law exposing instantaneous event rate where defined. |
| `LogProbabilityDensity` | class | A continuous law that evaluates natural log density. |
| `LogProbabilityMassFunction` | class | A discrete law that evaluates natural log probability mass. |
| `MultivariateLogDensity` | class | A continuous vector-valued law with a log density on ``R^d``. |
| `MultivariateLogMass` | class | A discrete vector-valued law with a log probability mass function. |
| `MultivariateMoments` | class | A vector-valued law exposing its mean vector and covariance matrix. |
| `MultivariateSampler` | class | A vector-valued law that draws reproducible samples. |
| `PACKAGE_STABILITY` | constant |  |
| `package_stability` | function | Return the stability label of a top-level package. |
| `ProbabilityDensity` | class | A continuous law that evaluates a probability density function. |
| `ProbabilityMassFunction` | class | A discrete law that assigns probability mass to candidate values. |
| `QuantileFunction` | class | A law exposing a generalized inverse cumulative distribution. |
| `RandomStream` | class | A named node in a tree of reproducible pseudo-random streams. |
| `request` | function | Build a validated :class:`EvaluationRequest` from any nested iterable. |
| `require_finite_complex` | function | Validate and return a finite complex number. |
| `require_finite_real` | function | Validate and return a finite real number. |
| `require_non_negative_finite_real` | function | Validate and return a finite non-negative real number. |
| `require_probability` | function | Validate and return a finite probability in the closed unit interval. |
| `resolve_stream` | function | Normalize ``None``, an integer seed, or a stream into a stream. |
| `run_procedure` | function | Run a procedure to completion with batch models supplied by the caller. |
| `scalar_outputs` | function | Coerce one scalar output per requested point to a tuple of floats. |
| `splitmix64` | function | Apply the SplitMix64 output finalizer to one 64-bit state. |
| `Stability` | constant |  |
| `SurvivalFunction` | class | A law exposing the direct upper-tail probability ``P(X>x)``. |
| `vector_outputs` | function | Coerce one output vector per requested point to tuples of floats. |

### `probstat.numerics` · stable

Dependency-free deterministic numerical algorithms and diagnostics.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `ACTIVATIONS` | constant |  |
| `admath` | class | Drop-in replacements for :mod:`math` that also accept :class:`Dual`. |
| `AutodiffUnsupportedError` | class | A dual number reached code that cannot propagate derivatives. |
| `compare_to_reference` | function | Measure a float implementation against a multiple-precision reference. |
| `composite_simpson` | function | Approximate a finite-interval integral by composite Simpson quadrature. |
| `composite_trapezoid` | function | Approximate a finite-interval integral by composite trapezoids. |
| `data_profile` | function | Build Moré-Wild data profiles from evaluations-to-solve counts. |
| `DataProfile` | class | Moré-Wild data profiles of several solvers on one problem set. |
| `DEQuadrature` | class | Tanh-sinh estimate with its convergence evidence. |
| `derivative` | function | Return ``f'(x)`` of a scalar function of one variable. |
| `digamma` | function | Return ``psi(x) = d/dx log Gamma(x)`` for ``x > 0``. |
| `Dual` | class | A (possibly nested) dual number ``value + tangent . eps``. |
| `EULER_GAMMA` | constant |  |
| `EVENT_LOCATIONS` | constant |  |
| `EventSolution` | class | State of an ordinary differential equation at a located event. |
| `exact_regularized_beta` | function | Return ``I_x(a, b)`` exactly for positive integer shapes. |
| `exp_sinh` | function | Integrate over a half-line ``[a, inf)`` or ``(-inf, b]`` by exp-sinh. |
| `first_converged_evaluation` | function | Return the evaluations needed to pass the Moré-Wild convergence test. |
| `FixedStepTrajectory` | class | Grid solution of an ordinary differential equation over a fixed horizon. |
| `gauss_kronrod` | function | Integrate ``f`` over ``[lower, upper]`` by adaptive G7-K15 bisection. |
| `gradient` | function | Return the exact gradient of a scalar function by forward mode. |
| `GRADIENT_OPTIONS` | constant |  |
| `GradientEvaluator` | class | Value-and-gradient oracle: analytic, autodiff, or finite differences. |
| `hessian` | function | Return the exact Hessian of a scalar function (forward over forward). |
| `hessian_vector_product` | function | Return ``H(x) v`` from one forward-over-reverse sweep. |
| `integrate` | function | Integrate ``f`` over ``[lower, upper]``, either limit possibly infinite. |
| `integrate_fixed_step` | function | Integrate ``y' = f(t, y)`` over ``steps`` fixed steps and keep every state. |
| `integrate_to_event` | function | Integrate ``y' = f(t, y)`` with a fixed step until ``g(t, y)`` reaches zero. |
| `IntegrationResult` | class | An integral estimate with its error estimate and convergence evidence. |
| `jacobian` | function | Return the Jacobian ``J_ij = d f_i / d x_j`` of a vector function. |
| `log_minus_digamma` | function | Return ``log x - psi(x)`` without cancellation for large ``x``. |
| `METHOD_ORDERS` | constant |  |
| `MLP` | class | Multilayer perceptron with a flat parameter vector. |
| `NonDifferentiableError` | class | A reverse-mode operation was evaluated where its derivative is unbounded. |
| `ODE_METHODS` | constant |  |
| `performance_profile` | function | Build Dolan-Moré performance profiles from a cost table. |
| `performance_ratios` | function | Return the performance ratios ``r_(p,s) = t_(p,s) / min_s t_(p,s)``. |
| `PerformanceProfile` | class | Dolan-Moré performance profiles of several solvers on one problem set. |
| `QuadratureResult` | class | A finite quadrature estimate and its deterministic work count. |
| `reference_erf` | function | Return the error function ``erf x`` rounded to ``digits`` digits. |
| `reference_erfc` | function | Return the complementary error function ``erfc x`` to ``digits`` digits. |
| `reference_exp` | function | Return ``exp(x)`` rounded to ``digits`` significant digits. |
| `reference_log` | function | Return the natural logarithm ``ln x`` rounded to ``digits`` digits. |
| `reference_log_gamma` | function | Return ``log Gamma(x)`` for ``x > 0`` rounded to ``digits`` digits. |
| `reference_normal_cdf` | function | Return the standard normal CDF ``Phi(x)`` rounded to ``digits`` digits. |
| `reference_normal_quantile` | function | Return the standard normal quantile ``Phi^-1(p)`` to ``digits`` digits. |
| `reference_pi` | function | Return ``pi`` rounded to ``digits`` significant digits. |
| `reference_regularized_beta` | function | Return the regularized incomplete beta function ``I_x(a, b)``. |
| `reference_regularized_gamma_p` | function | Return the regularized lower incomplete gamma ``P(a, x)``. |
| `reference_regularized_gamma_q` | function | Return the regularized upper incomplete gamma ``Q(a, x) = 1 - P(a, x)``. |
| `ReferenceComparison` | class | Errors of a float implementation against a multiple-precision reference. |
| `reverse_gradient` | function | Return the exact gradient of a scalar function by reverse mode. |
| `reverse_hessian` | function | Return the exact Hessian of a scalar function (forward over reverse). |
| `reverse_jacobian` | function | Return the Jacobian ``J_ij = d f_i / d x_j`` from one recording. |
| `reverse_value_and_gradient` | function | Return ``f(x)`` and ``grad f(x)`` from one recording and one sweep. |
| `reverse_value_gradient_and_hessian` | function | Return ``f(x)``, its gradient and its Hessian by forward over reverse. |
| `ReverseVariable` | class | A scalar recorded on a reverse-mode tape. |
| `sinh_sinh` | function | Integrate over the whole real line by sinh-sinh quadrature. |
| `tanh_sinh` | function | Integrate ``g(u)`` over ``(lower, upper)`` with ``0 <= lower < upper <= 1``. |
| `tanh_sinh_interval` | function | Integrate ``f`` over a finite ``[lower, upper]`` by tanh-sinh quadrature. |
| `trigamma` | function | Return ``psi'(x)`` for ``x > 0``. |
| `ulp_error` | function | Return ``\|value - reference\|`` in units in the last place of the reference. |
| `value_and_gradient` | function | Return ``f(x)`` and ``grad f(x)`` from one vector-tangent evaluation. |
| `value_gradient_and_hessian` | function | Return ``f(x)``, its gradient and its Hessian by nested forward mode. |
| `vector_jacobian_product` | function | Return ``w' J(x)`` of a vector function from one sweep. |

### `probstat.linalg` · stable

Dependency-free dense linear algebra shared by every higher layer.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `add` | function | Return ``left + scale * right`` for equally shaped matrices. |
| `adjoint_test` | function | Run the adjoint (dot-product) test of an operator. |
| `AdjointTestResult` | class | Outcome of the dot-product test ``y'(A x) = (A'y)'x``. |
| `apply` | function | Return ``A x`` for any :class:`LinearOperator`, validating the result. |
| `apply_transpose` | function | Return ``A'y`` for any :class:`LinearOperator`, validating the result. |
| `as_matrix` | function | Materialize and validate a non-empty rectangular finite matrix. |
| `as_vector` | function | Materialize and validate a finite real vector. |
| `available_backends` | function | Return the registered backend names (not a check that NumPy imports). |
| `BlockDiagonalCovariance` | class | Block-diagonal covariance ``diag(Gamma_1, ..., Gamma_k)`` of independent blocks. |
| `BlockDiagonalOperator` | class | Block-diagonal operator ``diag(A_1, ..., A_k)``. |
| `CallbackOperator` | class | Operator defined by user callbacks for ``A x`` and ``A'y``. |
| `cgls` | function | Solve ``min \|\|b - A x\|\|^2 + damp^2 \|\|x\|\|^2`` by CGLS. |
| `cholesky` | function | Return the lower Cholesky factor of a positive-definite matrix. |
| `cholesky_semidefinite` | function | Return a lower square root of a positive-semidefinite matrix. |
| `cholesky_solve` | function | Solve ``L L' x = b`` from a precomputed Cholesky factor. |
| `CholeskyFactorization` | class | A retained factorization ``A + delta I = L L'`` and its solves. |
| `ComposedOperator` | class | The product ``A_1 A_2 ... A_k`` (``A_k`` applied first). |
| `conjugate_gradient` | function | Solve ``M x = b`` for a symmetric positive-definite operator ``M`` by CG. |
| `Convolution1DOperator` | class | Discrete 1D convolution ``(K x)_i = sum_l k_l x_(i + l - c)`` on ``n`` points. |
| `CountingOperator` | class | Wrapper that counts forward and transpose applications separately. |
| `DEFAULT_JACOBI_TOLERANCE` | constant |  |
| `DEFAULT_RANK_TOLERANCE` | constant |  |
| `DenseBackend` | class | The dense kernels that the ``n^3`` consumers of the library need. |
| `DenseCovariance` | class | Dense SPD covariance held by its lower Cholesky factor ``Gamma = L L'``. |
| `DenseOperator` | class | Operator of an explicit dense ``m x n`` matrix. |
| `determinant` | function | Return ``det A`` from a partial-pivot LU factorization. |
| `DiagonalCovariance` | class | Diagonal covariance ``diag(s_1^2, ..., s_m^2)`` with factor ``diag(s)``. |
| `DiagonalOperator` | class | Square diagonal operator ``diag(d)``. |
| `dot` | function | Return the compensated Euclidean inner product. |
| `FirstDifferenceOperator` | class | Scaled first difference ``(D x)_i = (x_(i+1) - x_i) / h`` in one dimension. |
| `get_backend` | function | Return the active dense backend (initially :class:`PythonBackend`). |
| `GridGradientOperator` | class | Forward-difference gradient of a 2D grid function stored row-major. |
| `householder_qr` | function | Return the thin Householder QR factorization of a tall matrix. |
| `HouseholderQR` | class | Immutable thin Householder factorization ``A = Q R`` of an ``n x p`` matrix. |
| `HStackOperator` | class | Column stacking ``[A_1, ..., A_k]`` of operators with equal range. |
| `hutchinson_trace` | function | Estimate ``tr(M)`` with independent Rademacher probes. |
| `identity` | function | Return the ``size`` by ``size`` identity matrix. |
| `IdentityOperator` | class | The identity ``I_n``. |
| `inverse_spd` | function | Invert a symmetric positive-definite matrix by Cholesky solves. |
| `is_covariance_factor` | function | Return whether ``value`` is one of this module's covariance factors. |
| `KrylovResult` | class | Result of a matrix-free least-squares or SPD solve. |
| `lanczos_eigenpairs` | function | Return Ritz pairs of a symmetric operator after ``steps`` Lanczos steps. |
| `LanczosResult` | class | Ritz pairs of a symmetric operator from ``k`` Lanczos steps. |
| `LinearOperator` | class | Structural protocol of a real linear map ``A: R^n -> R^m``. |
| `log_abs_determinant` | function | Return ``log \|det A\|`` from a partial-pivot LU factorization. |
| `log_det_spd` | function | Return ``log det A`` for a symmetric positive-definite matrix. |
| `lsmr` | function | Solve ``min \|\|b - A x\|\|^2 + damp^2 \|\|x\|\|^2`` by LSMR. |
| `lsqr` | function | Solve ``min \|\|b - A x\|\|^2 + damp^2 \|\|x\|\|^2`` by LSQR. |
| `lu_solve` | function | Solve a general square system by Gaussian elimination with pivoting. |
| `materialize` | function | Return the dense matrix of an operator by applying it to unit vectors. |
| `matmul` | function | Multiply two compatible matrices. |
| `Matrix` | constant |  |
| `matvec` | function | Multiply a matrix by a compatible vector. |
| `norm` | function | Return the Euclidean norm without intermediate overflow. |
| `numerical_rank` | function | Return the number of singular values above ``tolerance * sigma_1``. |
| `NumPyBackend` | class | Optional backend on NumPy's LAPACK and BLAS kernels. |
| `OperatorBase` | class | Validated base class of the concrete operators in this module. |
| `OperatorCounts` | class | Numbers of forward and transpose applications of a counted operator. |
| `outer` | function | Return the outer product ``x y'``. |
| `PermutationOperator` | class | Permutation ``(P x)_i = x_(p_i)`` with ``P' = P^-1``. |
| `power_iteration` | function | Estimate ``\|\|A\|\|_2`` by power iteration on ``A'A``. |
| `pseudo_inverse` | function | Return the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse with a relative cut-off. |
| `PythonBackend` | class | Dependency-free reference backend (the default). |
| `quadratic_form` | function | Return ``x' A x``. |
| `randomized_svd` | function | Return a rank-``k`` approximate SVD by the randomized range finder. |
| `RandomizedSVD` | class | Approximate truncated SVD ``A ~= U diag(sigma) V'`` from a random range. |
| `require_operator` | function | Return ``operator`` after checking that it is a linear operator. |
| `sandwich` | function | Return the congruence ``H S H'`` used for linear covariance propagation. |
| `scale_matrix` | function | Multiply every matrix entry by a finite scalar. |
| `ScaledIdentityCovariance` | class | Isotropic covariance ``s^2 I_m``. |
| `ScaledOperator` | class | The operator ``c A`` for a finite scalar ``c``. |
| `SelectionOperator` | class | Restriction ``(S x)_i = x_(k_i)`` to selected indices. |
| `set_backend` | function | Select the dense backend for the whole process. |
| `singular_value_decomposition` | function | Return the thin SVD ``A = U diag(sigma) V'`` by one-sided Jacobi. |
| `SingularValueDecomposition` | class | Immutable thin singular value decomposition ``A = U diag(sigma) V'``. |
| `solve_lower` | function | Solve ``L x = b`` by forward substitution. |
| `solve_spd` | function | Solve a symmetric positive-definite system through Cholesky factors. |
| `solve_upper` | function | Solve ``U x = b`` by back substitution. |
| `SpectralNormEstimate` | class | Power-iteration estimate of ``\|\|A\|\|_2``. |
| `SumOperator` | class | The sum ``A_1 + ... + A_k`` of operators of equal shape. |
| `symmetric_eigen` | function | Diagonalize a real symmetric matrix by cyclic Jacobi rotations. |
| `SymmetricPositiveDefinite` | class | Structural protocol of an SPD metric ``M`` with apply and solve actions. |
| `symmetrize` | function | Validate approximate symmetry and return the averaged symmetric matrix. |
| `TraceEstimate` | class | Hutchinson estimate of ``tr(M)``. |
| `transpose` | function | Return the transpose of a rectangular matrix. |
| `TransposedOperator` | class | The transpose ``A'`` of an operator. |
| `use_backend` | function | Activate a dense backend for the duration of a ``with`` block. |
| `Vector` | constant |  |
| `VStackOperator` | class | Row stacking ``[A_1; ...; A_k]`` of operators with equal domain. |
| `weighted_adjoint` | function | Return the adjoint ``A* = M_X^-1 A' M_Y`` in weighted inner products. |
| `ZeroOperator` | class | The zero map ``R^n -> R^m``. |

### `probstat.optimization` · stable

Dependency-free optimization, root finding, and numerical differentiation.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `adam_minimize` | function | Minimize a stochastic objective with Adam. |
| `AdamResult` | class | Outcome of :func:`adam_minimize`. |
| `admm` | function | Solve ``min f(x) + g(z)`` subject to ``L x = z`` by scaled ADMM. |
| `ADMMResult` | class | Outcome of :func:`admm`. |
| `BoxTransform` | class | Smooth map between unconstrained coordinates ``z`` and a box for ``x``. |
| `brent_root` | function | Find a root in a sign-changing bracket by Brent's method. |
| `central_gradient` | function | Approximate a gradient by central differences with one-sided fallback. |
| `central_hessian` | function | Approximate a symmetric Hessian by second-order central differences. |
| `central_jacobian` | function | Approximate the Jacobian of a vector function by central differences. |
| `fista` | function | Minimise ``f(x) + g(x)`` by FISTA with backtracking and optional restart. |
| `forward_gradient` | function | Approximate a gradient by forward differences with backward fallback. |
| `gauss_newton_covariance` | function | Return the Gauss-Newton parameter covariance ``s^2 (J'J)^-1``. |
| `ista` | function | Minimise ``f(x) + g(x)`` by the proximal gradient method (ISTA). |
| `least_squares` | function | Minimize ``0.5 \|\|r(x)\|\|^2`` by Levenberg-Marquardt with Marquardt scaling. |
| `LeastSquaresResult` | class | Outcome of a nonlinear least-squares fit. |
| `minimize_bfgs` | function | Minimize a smooth function by BFGS with a strong-Wolfe line search. |
| `minimize_nelder_mead` | function | Minimize a function without derivatives by the Nelder-Mead simplex. |
| `multi_start` | function | Run a local minimizer from several seeded random starts in a box. |
| `MultiStartResult` | class | Outcome of a seeded multi-start minimization. |
| `OptimizationResult` | class | Outcome of an unconstrained minimization. |
| `PDHGResult` | class | Outcome of :func:`primal_dual_hybrid_gradient`. |
| `primal_dual_hybrid_gradient` | function | Solve ``min_x f(x) + g(K x)`` by the Chambolle-Pock iteration. |
| `project_box` | function | Return the Euclidean projection onto ``{lower <= x <= upper}``. |
| `project_l2_ball` | function | Return the projection onto ``{\|\|x\|\|_2 <= radius}``. |
| `project_linf_ball` | function | Return the projection onto ``{\|x_i\| <= r_i}`` (clipping). |
| `project_pointwise_l2_ball` | function | Return the pointwise projection onto the Euclidean balls ``\|p_i\| <= r_i``. |
| `prox_elastic_net` | function | Return the prox of ``step (l1 sum w_i \|x_i\| + l2/2 \|\|x\|\|^2)``. |
| `prox_group_l2` | function | Return the prox of the group penalty ``step sum_g w_g \|\|x_g\|\|_2``. |
| `prox_l1` | function | Return ``argmin_x step sum_i w_i \|x_i - c_i\| + 1/2\|\|x - v\|\|^2`` on a box. |
| `prox_nonnegative_l1` | function | Return the prox of ``step sum w_i x_i + indicator(x >= 0)``. |
| `prox_pointwise_l2` | function | Return the prox of ``step sum_i w_i \|p_i\|`` with ``\|.\|`` the 2-norm. |
| `ProximalGradientResult` | class | Outcome of :func:`ista` or :func:`fista`. |
| `ProxMap` | constant |  |
| `RootResult` | class | Outcome of a bracketing scalar root search. |
| `soft_threshold` | function | Return the soft-thresholding ``sign(v) max(\|v\| - theta, 0)``. |
| `steihaug_cg` | function | Solve ``min g'p + 1/2 p'Hp`` over ``\|\|p\|\| <= radius`` by truncated CG. |
| `SteihaugResult` | class | Result of :func:`steihaug_cg`. |
| `trust_region_newton_cg` | function | Minimize a smooth function by trust-region Newton-CG (Steihaug). |
| `TrustRegionIteration` | class | One attempt of :func:`trust_region_newton_cg`. |
| `TrustRegionResult` | class | Result of :func:`trust_region_newton_cg`. |

### `probstat.sampling` · stable

Space-filling and low-discrepancy sampling designs on the unit hypercube.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `cbc_generating_vector` | function | Construct a generating vector component by component (greedy). |
| `halton` | function | Return a Halton low-discrepancy design, optionally randomized. |
| `HALTON_MAX_DIMENSION` | constant |  |
| `iman_conover` | function | Rearrange each column of a design to induce a target rank correlation. |
| `korobov_generating_vector` | function | Search the Korobov vectors ``(1, a, ..., a^(s-1))`` for the best ``a``. |
| `korobov_vector` | function | Return the Korobov vector ``(1, a, a^2, ..., a^(s-1)) mod n``. |
| `latin_hypercube` | function | Return a Latin hypercube design with one point per marginal stratum. |
| `lattice_squared_error` | function | Return the shift-averaged squared worst-case error of a lattice rule. |
| `LatticeRule` | class | A constructed rank-1 lattice rule with its quality criterion. |
| `maximin_criterion` | function | Return the Morris-Mitchell criterion ``phi_p = (sum_(i<j) d_ij^-p)^(1/p)``. |
| `maximin_latin_hypercube` | function | Return a Latin hypercube improved for the Morris-Mitchell maximin criterion. |
| `median_random_lattice` | function | Estimate an integral by the median of randomly generated lattice rules. |
| `monte_carlo_design` | function | Return independent uniform points (plain Monte Carlo). |
| `radical_inverse` | function | Return the (optionally digit-permuted) radical inverse of an integer. |
| `rank1_lattice` | function | Return the rank-1 lattice ``x_k = frac(k z / n)``, optionally shifted. |
| `rqmc_mean` | function | Estimate ``int_[0,1]^s f`` by the mean of randomized QMC replicates. |
| `rqmc_median` | function | Estimate ``int_[0,1]^s f`` by the median of randomized QMC replicates. |
| `RQMC_METHODS` | constant |  |
| `RQMCResult` | class | An integral estimate from independently randomized quasi-Monte Carlo rules. |
| `SamplingDesign` | class | An immutable point set on the unit hypercube with provenance. |
| `sobol` | function | Return a Sobol digital-net design in Gray-code order (up to 1024 dimensions). |
| `SOBOL_MAX_DIMENSION` | constant |  |
| `SobolBalanceWarning` | class | Warn that a Sobol design size is not a power of two. |
| `star_discrepancy_bound` | function | Return a grid-based lower bound on the star discrepancy of a design. |

## Probability and statistics

Exact finite probability, distribution families, descriptive and test statistics, multivariate laws, stochastic processes, and information measures.

### `probstat.probability` · stable

Finite sample spaces, probability and general finite measures, and counting.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `bernoulli_summand` | function | Return the Bernoulli(``p``) summand, whose sums are binomial. |
| `bernstein_bound` | function | Return Bernstein's ``exp(-n t^2 / (2 (sigma^2 + c t / 3)))``. |
| `berry_esseen_bound` | function | Return the Berry-Esseen bound ``C rho / (sigma^3 sqrt(n))``. |
| `BERRY_ESSEEN_CONSTANT` | constant |  |
| `chebyshev_bound` | function | Return Chebyshev's ``P(\|Xbar_n - mu\| >= t) <= sigma^2 / (n t^2)``. |
| `check_completeness` | function | Check completeness of a statistic for a finite parametric family. |
| `clt_error_profile` | function | Tabulate exact CLT and Edgeworth errors against the Berry-Esseen bound. |
| `CLTErrorRow` | class | Exact central-limit error at one sample size. |
| `combinations` | function | Return unordered selections of ``r`` objects from ``n`` distinct objects. |
| `compare_tail_bounds` | function | Compare exact sample-mean tails with Chebyshev, Hoeffding, Bernstein. |
| `CompletenessCheck` | class | Result of the finite-family completeness check of a statistic. |
| `delta_method_bernoulli` | function | Compare ``Var g(Xbar)`` for Bernoulli data with the delta method. |
| `delta_method_exponential_reciprocal` | function | Compare ``Var(1 / Xbar)`` for exponential data with the delta method. |
| `delta_method_normal_exp` | function | Compare ``Var(exp(Xbar))`` for normal data with the delta method. |
| `delta_method_normal_square` | function | Compare ``Var(Xbar^2)`` for normal data with the delta method. |
| `delta_method_variance` | function | Return the delta-method variance ``g'(mu)^2 sigma^2 / n`` of ``g(Xbar)``. |
| `DeltaMethodComparison` | class | Exact variance of ``g(Xbar_n)`` next to its delta-method approximation. |
| `edgeworth_cdf` | function | Return the one-term Edgeworth approximation to ``P(Z_n <= z)``. |
| `exponential_summand` | function | Return the Exponential(``rate``) summand, whose sums are gamma. |
| `factorial` | function | Return the number of orderings of ``n`` distinct objects. |
| `FiniteEvent` | class | An immutable subset of a particular finite sample space. |
| `FiniteJointDistribution` | class | The induced joint law of two variables on one finite measure. |
| `FiniteMeasurableSpace` | class | A finite set with the sigma-algebra generated by chosen subsets. |
| `FiniteMeasure` | class | A non-negative measure on a finite measurable space. |
| `FiniteProbabilityMeasure` | class | A validated probability measure on a finite sample space. |
| `FiniteRandomVariable` | class | An immutable measurable mapping on a finite probability measure. |
| `FiniteSampleSpace` | class | A non-empty finite set of distinct, hashable outcomes. |
| `FubiniCheck` | class | Product integral and both iterated integrals of one function. |
| `hoeffding_bound` | function | Return Hoeffding's bound ``exp(-2 n t^2 / (b - a)^2)``. |
| `is_complete_statistic` | function | Return whether a statistic is complete for a finite family. |
| `is_sigma_algebra` | function | Return whether a collection of subsets is a sigma-algebra. |
| `kolmogorov_distance_to_normal` | function | Return the exact ``sup_z \|P(Z_n <= z) - A_n(z)\|``. |
| `multiplication_rule` | function | Return the count for a sequence of finite independent choices. |
| `normal_summand` | function | Return the Normal(``mean``, ``sd^2``) summand, whose sums are normal. |
| `permutations` | function | Return ordered selections of ``r`` objects from ``n`` distinct objects. |
| `sample_mean_two_sided_tail` | function | Return the exact two-sided tail ``P(\|Xbar_n - mu\| >= t)``. |
| `sample_mean_upper_tail` | function | Return the exact one-sided tail ``P(Xbar_n - mu >= t)``. |
| `standardized_mean_cdf` | function | Return the exact ``P(Z_n <= z)`` from the closed-form law of the sum. |
| `SummandLaw` | class | Moments and support of one i.i.d. summand with a closed-form sum law. |
| `TailBoundComparison` | class | Exact sample-mean tails next to distribution-free bounds. |

### `probstat.distributions` · stable

Validated probability-distribution objects and support descriptors.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `Affine` | class | The law of ``X = loc + scale Y`` for a continuous base law of ``Y``. |
| `Bernoulli` | class | A Bernoulli distribution with success probability ``p``. |
| `Beta` | class | A beta law with shapes ``alpha`` and ``beta`` on ``[0, 1]``. |
| `Binomial` | class | The number of successes in ``n`` independent Bernoulli trials. |
| `Categorical` | class | An unordered finite distribution over distinct hashable labels. |
| `Cauchy` | class | The Cauchy law with location ``loc`` and scale ``scale``. |
| `ChiSquare` | class | A chi-square law with ``nu`` degrees of freedom. |
| `ContinuousDistribution` | class | Mixin with consistent defaults for continuous univariate laws. |
| `DiscreteDistribution` | class | Mixin with consistent defaults for integer-valued laws. |
| `DiscreteUniform` | class | The discrete uniform law on the integers ``low, low + 1, ..., high``. |
| `Empirical` | class | The empirical law of a finite sample (see the module docstring). |
| `Exponential` | class | An exponential waiting-time law with positive rate ``rate``. |
| `FiniteSupport` | class | A non-empty ordered collection of distinct hashable support values. |
| `FisherF` | class | The F law of ``(V_1/nu1) / (V_2/nu2)`` for independent chi-squares. |
| `Gamma` | class | A gamma law with shape ``k`` and rate ``lambda`` (scale ``1/lambda``). |
| `GeneralizedExtremeValue` | class | A GEV law ``F(x) = exp(-(1 + xi z)^(-1/xi))``, ``z = (x - mu)/sigma``. |
| `GeneralizedPareto` | class | A GPD law ``S(x) = (1 + xi z)^(-1/xi)``, ``z = (x - mu)/sigma >= 0``. |
| `Geometric` | class | Failures before the first success in independent Bernoulli trials. |
| `Gumbel` | class | A Gumbel law for maxima (``kind="max"``) or minima (``kind="min"``). |
| `Hypergeometric` | class | Successes in ``draws`` draws without replacement from a finite population. |
| `IntegerSupport` | class | A bounded or unbounded inclusive integer support. |
| `Laplace` | class | The Laplace (double exponential) law ``f = exp(-\|x - loc\|/b) / (2 b)``. |
| `Logistic` | class | A logistic law with ``F(x) = 1 / (1 + exp(-(x - mu)/s))``. |
| `LogNormal` | class | A lognormal law parameterized by ``log(X) ~ Normal(mu, sigma)``. |
| `maximum_entropy` | function | Return the maximum-entropy law on ``[lower, upper]`` under the constraints. |
| `MaximumEntropy` | class | Maximum-entropy law ``f(x) = 1_K(x) exp(-lambda_0 - sum lambda_m g_m(x))``. |
| `MaximumEntropyDiagnostics` | class | Convergence record of a maximum-entropy solve. |
| `Mixture` | class | A finite mixture ``f = sum_i w_i f_i`` of continuous laws. |
| `NegativeBinomial` | class | Failures before the ``r``-th success in independent Bernoulli trials. |
| `Normal` | class | A univariate normal distribution parameterized by mean and scale. |
| `Pareto` | class | The Pareto type I law ``S(x) = (x_m / x)^alpha`` on ``x >= x_m``. |
| `PERT` | class | The beta-PERT law on ``[low, high]`` with most likely value ``mode``. |
| `Poisson` | class | A Poisson count distribution with finite non-negative rate ``lam``. |
| `Rayleigh` | class | The Rayleigh law ``f(x) = x / s^2 exp(-x^2 / (2 s^2))`` on ``x >= 0``. |
| `RealSupport` | class | An interval support over the extended real line. |
| `StudentT` | class | A Student-t law ``X = mu + sigma T`` with ``T ~ t(nu)``. |
| `Triangular` | class | A triangular law with lower end ``a``, mode ``c``, and upper end ``b``. |
| `Truncated` | class | A continuous law conditioned on ``lower <= X <= upper`` (one side may be open). |
| `TruncatedNormal` | class | A ``Normal(mu, sigma)`` law conditioned on ``lower <= X <= upper``. |
| `Uniform` | class | A continuous uniform law on ``[lower, upper]``. |
| `Weibull` | class | A Weibull law with shape ``k`` and scale ``scale``. |

### `probstat.statistics` · stable

Descriptive statistics, one-pass streaming summaries, and historical data sets.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `BandwidthSelection` | class | Outcome of a cross-validated bandwidth search. |
| `BORTKIEWICZ_HORSE_KICKS` | constant |  |
| `catoni_influence` | function | Return Catoni's influence function ``sign(x) log(1 + \|x\| + x^2/2)``. |
| `catoni_mean` | function | Return Catoni's M-estimator of the mean. |
| `catoni_scale` | function | Return the default scale ``a = sqrt(2 log(1/delta) / (n v))``. |
| `CatoniMeanResult` | class | Catoni M-estimate of a mean with the root-search diagnostics. |
| `chatterjee_correlation` | function | Return Chatterjee's rank correlation ``xi_n`` of ``y`` on ``x``. |
| `ChatterjeeResult` | class | Chatterjee's rank correlation with its independence test. |
| `covariance` | function | Return covariance for two equally sized finite samples. |
| `DataColumn` | class | One named variable of a data set with its unit. |
| `DataSet` | class | A frozen historical data set with provenance and licence. |
| `dataset_by_key` | function | Return a bundled data set by its key. |
| `DATASETS` | constant |  |
| `distance_correlation` | function | Return the sample distance covariance and distance correlation. |
| `distance_correlation_bivariate_normal` | function | Return the population distance correlation of a bivariate normal pair. |
| `DistanceCorrelationResult` | class | Sample distance covariance and correlation with an optional test. |
| `empirical_cdf` | function | Evaluate the empirical cumulative distribution function at ``x``. |
| `ExceedanceCounter` | class | Mergeable streaming event or exceedance proportion with a Wilson interval. |
| `excess_kurtosis` | function | Return the sample excess kurtosis under one of three Joanes-Gill conventions. |
| `gaussian_kde` | function | Return a Gaussian kernel density estimate of a univariate sample. |
| `gaussian_kde_multivariate` | function | Return a Gaussian KDE whose kernel covariance is ``h^2 R``. |
| `interquartile_range` | function | Return the type-7 interquartile range ``Q(0.75) - Q(0.25)``. |
| `kde_bandwidth` | function | Return a rule-of-thumb or cross-validated bandwidth for a Gaussian kernel. |
| `kde_bcv` | function | Return the biased cross-validation score ``BCV(h)``. |
| `kde_loo_log_likelihood` | function | Return the cross-validated log-likelihood of a Gaussian KDE bandwidth. |
| `kde_ucv` | function | Return the unbiased (least-squares) cross-validation score ``UCV(h)``. |
| `KernelDensity` | class | Gaussian kernel density estimate ``f_h(x) = (1/nh) sum phi((x - x_i)/h)``. |
| `ledoit_wolf_covariance` | function | Return the Ledoit-Wolf shrinkage estimate of a covariance matrix. |
| `LedoitWolfResult` | class | Shrinkage covariance estimate with the quantities that define it. |
| `LIEBLEIN_ZELEN_BALL_BEARINGS` | constant |  |
| `mean` | function | Return the arithmetic mean of a non-empty finite sample. |
| `median` | function | Return the sample median (type-7 quantile at one half). |
| `median_absolute_deviation` | function | Return the median absolute deviation from the median. |
| `median_of_means` | function | Return the median of the means of ``k`` disjoint blocks. |
| `median_of_means_block_count` | function | Return the block count ``k = ceil(8 log(1/delta))``. |
| `MedianOfMeansResult` | class | Median-of-means estimate with its block structure. |
| `multivariate_kde_factor` | function | Return a rule-of-thumb bandwidth factor for a covariance-shaped kernel. |
| `multivariate_kde_loo_log_likelihood` | function | Return the leave-one-out log-likelihood of a bandwidth factor. |
| `MultivariateKernelDensity` | class | Gaussian KDE ``f_h(x) = (1/N) sum_i phi_d(x - x_i; h^2 R)``. |
| `NEWCOMB_PASSAGE_TIMES` | constant |  |
| `oversmoothed_bandwidth` | function | Return ``h_os = 1.144 s n^(-1/5)``, the upper end of R's search interval. |
| `P2Quantile` | class | Fixed-memory streaming estimate of one quantile (the P² algorithm). |
| `pearson_correlation` | function | Return the Pearson product-moment correlation coefficient. |
| `quantile` | function | Return a sample quantile under an explicit Hyndman-Fan definition. |
| `quantiles` | function | Return several sample quantiles from one sort. |
| `ranks` | function | Return one-based ranks with ties assigned their average rank. |
| `ReservoirSampler` | class | Exact uniform random subset of fixed size from a stream (Algorithm R). |
| `run_until` | function | Draw samples into a running mean until a relative error is reached. |
| `RunningCovariance` | class | Mergeable one-pass mean vector, covariance, and correlation matrix. |
| `RunningMoments` | class | Mergeable one-pass count, mean, variance, skewness, and kurtosis. |
| `RUTHERFORD_GEIGER_SCINTILLATIONS` | constant |  |
| `select_kde_bandwidth` | function | Select a Gaussian-kernel bandwidth by cross-validation. |
| `skewness` | function | Return the sample skewness under one of three Joanes-Gill conventions. |
| `spearman_correlation` | function | Return Spearman's rank correlation (Pearson correlation of ranks). |
| `standard_deviation` | function | Return the non-negative square root of finite-sample variance. |
| `StreamingRunResult` | class | Outcome of :func:`run_until`, a relative-error-targeted running mean. |
| `STUDENT_SLEEP` | constant |  |
| `trimmed_mean` | function | Return the symmetric trimmed mean. |
| `variance` | function | Return finite-sample variance with an explicit degrees-of-freedom offset. |
| `WeightedRunningMoments` | class | Mergeable one-pass weighted mean and variance (West's algorithm). |
| `z_scores` | function | Standardize observations relative to their sample center and spread. |

### `probstat.multivariate` · provisional

Multivariate probability distributions and vector-valued operations.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `BallProbability` | class | Probability content of a ball with an error statement. |
| `delta_method` | function | Propagate a mean and covariance through ``g`` by linearization. |
| `DeltaMethodResult` | class | First-order moment propagation through a smooth vector map. |
| `Dirichlet` | class | The Dirichlet law ``Dir(alpha)`` on the ``(K-1)``-simplex. |
| `dirichlet_posterior` | function | Update a Dirichlet prior with observed category counts. |
| `DirichletMultinomial` | class | The compound (Polya) law ``DirMult(n, alpha)`` of category counts. |
| `DirichletPosterior` | class | Result of a Dirichlet-categorical (multinomial) conjugate update. |
| `gaussian_ball_probability` | function | Return ``P(\|\|X\|\| <= R)`` for ``X ~ N(mu, Sigma)`` in ``n`` dimensions. |
| `gaussian_disc_probability` | function | Return ``P(\|\|X\|\| <= R)`` for a two-dimensional Gaussian ``X``. |
| `InverseWishart` | class | The inverse-Wishart law ``InverseWishart_p(Psi, nu)``. |
| `isotropic_gaussian_ball_probability` | function | Return ``P(\|\|X\|\| <= R)`` for ``X ~ N(mu, sigma^2 I)`` with ``\|\|mu\|\| = offset``. |
| `lkj_log_normalizer` | function | Return ``log c_d(eta)``, the log normalizing constant of LKJ(eta). |
| `LKJCholesky` | class | The LKJ(eta) law of the Cholesky factor ``L`` of a correlation matrix. |
| `LKJCorrelation` | class | The LKJ(eta) law on ``d x d`` correlation matrices. |
| `Matrix` | constant |  |
| `maximum_disc_probability` | function | Return the largest disc probability over a covariance scale factor. |
| `MaximumBallProbability` | class | Largest ball probability over a covariance scale factor. |
| `Multinomial` | class | Counts of ``n`` independent categorical trials, ``Mult(n, p)``. |
| `multivariate_log_gamma` | function | Return the multivariate log-gamma function ``log Gamma_p(a)``. |
| `MultivariateNormal` | class | A non-degenerate multivariate normal distribution. |
| `MultivariateStudentT` | class | The ``p``-variate Student-t law ``t_nu(mu, Sigma)``. |
| `noncentral_chi_square_cdf` | function | Return the noncentral chi-square distribution function. |
| `normal_inverse_wishart_posterior` | function | Update a normal-inverse-Wishart prior with multivariate normal data. |
| `NormalInverseWishart` | class | Normal-inverse-Wishart law of a mean vector and covariance matrix. |
| `NormalInverseWishartPosterior` | class | Result of a normal-inverse-Wishart conjugate update. |
| `small_disc_probability` | function | Return the small-disc approximation ``pi R^2 f(0)`` and its error. |
| `transformed_density` | function | Build the log-density of ``Y = g(X)`` by the change-of-variables rule. |
| `TransformedDensity` | class | Density of ``Y = g(X)`` for a smooth bijection ``g`` with known inverse. |
| `Vector` | constant |  |
| `Wishart` | class | The Wishart law ``Wishart_p(S, nu)`` on positive-definite matrices. |

### `probstat.processes` · provisional

Stochastic processes and Gaussian random fields.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `AbsorptionAnalysis` | class | Fundamental-matrix summary of an absorbing Markov chain. |
| `as_points` | function | Normalize scalar 1-D locations or coordinate tuples into points. |
| `BoundedValue` | class | A computed value together with a rigorous absolute error bound. |
| `brownian_increments` | function | Return the increments of standard Brownian motion on a uniform grid. |
| `BrownianMotion` | class | Brownian motion with drift ``X_t = x_0 + mu t + sigma W_t``. |
| `CompoundPoissonProcess` | class | Compound Poisson process ``S(t) = sum_(i <= N(t)) J_i``. |
| `ContinuousTimeMarkovChain` | class | Homogeneous continuous-time Markov chain on finitely many labelled states. |
| `ConvergenceStudy` | class | Monte Carlo estimate of strong or weak discretization error. |
| `covariance_matrix` | function | Return the covariance matrix ``C_ij = C(x_i, x_j)`` on a point set. |
| `CovarianceKernel` | class | Stationary isotropic covariance kernel ``C(x, y) = sigma^2 rho(r)``. |
| `CTMCAbsorption` | class | Absorption analysis of a CTMC whose closed classes are absorbing states. |
| `CTMCPath` | class | One simulated CTMC path on ``[0, horizon]``. |
| `DIFFUSION_MODELS` | constant |  |
| `DiffusionSample` | class | Points produced by a reverse-time sampler. |
| `empirical_order` | function | Return the least-squares slope ``q`` in ``log e = log C + q log h``. |
| `euler_maruyama` | function | Integrate a scalar SDE by the Euler-Maruyama scheme. |
| `exponential_kernel_kl_modes` | function | Return the leading exact KL eigenpairs of an exponential kernel. |
| `ExponentialKLMode` | class | One analytic KL eigenpair of the exponential kernel on ``[-a, a]``. |
| `fit_neural_sde` | function | Fit a neural SDE to observed trajectories by Euler pseudo-likelihood. |
| `ForwardSDE` | class | Forward diffusion with an affine Gaussian perturbation kernel. |
| `gauss_legendre` | function | Return Gauss-Legendre nodes and weights on ``[lower, upper]``. |
| `GaussianScore` | class | Exact score of Gaussian data diffused by a forward SDE. |
| `GeometricBrownianMotion` | class | Geometric Brownian motion ``dS = mu S dt + sigma S dW``. |
| `interval_rule` | function | Return quadrature nodes and weights for a 1-D interval. |
| `JumpLaw` | class | Structural interface for the jump-size law of a compound process. |
| `karhunen_loeve` | function | Compute a truncated KL expansion by the Nystrom method. |
| `karhunen_loeve_interval` | function | Compute a Nystrom KL expansion on a 1-D interval with a quadrature rule. |
| `KarhunenLoeveExpansion` | class | Truncated discrete Karhunen-Loeve expansion of a Gaussian field. |
| `KERNEL_FAMILIES` | constant |  |
| `MarkovChain` | class | Homogeneous discrete-time Markov chain on a finite state space. |
| `milstein` | function | Integrate a scalar SDE by the Milstein scheme. |
| `NeuralSDE` | class | Diagonal-noise SDE with an MLP drift and a constant or MLP diffusion. |
| `NeuralSDEFit` | class | A :class:`NeuralSDE` with parameters fitted by pseudo-likelihood. |
| `OccupancySolution` | class | Expected time spent in each state over ``[0, t]``. |
| `ODE_METHODS` | constant |  |
| `OrnsteinUhlenbeck` | class | Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process ``dX = theta (mu - X) dt + sigma dW``. |
| `perturb` | function | Draw ``x_t ~ N(m(t) x_0, v(t) I)`` from the perturbation kernel. |
| `perturbation_moments` | function | Return the mean and per-coordinate variance of ``x_t \| x_0``. |
| `PoissonProcess` | class | Homogeneous Poisson counting process ``N(t)`` with rate ``lambda``. |
| `predictor_corrector_sample` | function | Sample with a reverse-SDE predictor and a Langevin corrector. |
| `probability_flow_map` | function | Transport points along the probability-flow ODE between two times. |
| `probability_flow_sample` | function | Sample by decoding prior draws with the probability-flow ODE. |
| `QUADRATURE_RULES` | constant |  |
| `rectangle_rule` | function | Return a tensor-product quadrature on a rectangle. |
| `RenewalCurve` | class | Expected reward ``E_alpha[r(Z_t)]`` on a uniform time grid. |
| `reverse_sde_sample` | function | Sample by integrating the reverse-time SDE with Euler-Maruyama. |
| `SamplePath` | class | One realization of a scalar process on an increasing time grid. |
| `ScalarSDE` | class | Scalar Ito SDE ``dX = a(t, X) dt + b(t, X) dW``. |
| `SCHEMES` | constant |  |

| `SemiMarkovAbsorption` | class | Exact absorption analysis of a semi-Markov process [EQ-PROC-SMP-004]. |
| `SemiMarkovEstimate` | class | A value from the discretised Markov renewal equation with its error estimate. |
| `SemiMarkovMonteCarlo` | class | Monte Carlo estimate of ``P(Z_t in S)`` at several times. |
| `SemiMarkovProcess` | class | Homogeneous semi-Markov process on finitely many labelled states. |
| `SemiMarkovTransient` | class | State law ``pi(t) = alpha phi(t)`` of a semi-Markov process. |
| `SemiMarkovTransitionMatrix` | class | Interval transition probabilities ``phi_ij(t)`` [EQ-PROC-SMP-002]. |
| `solve_sde` | function | Integrate a scalar SDE on a uniform grid. |

| `train_score_network` | function | Fit a time-conditioned score network by denoising score matching. |
| `TrainedScore` | class | Score network with fitted parameters; callable as ``score(x, t)``. |
| `TransientSolution` | class | Transient state law ``pi(t)`` of a CTMC computed by uniformization. |
| `uniform_grid` | function | Return ``steps + 1`` equally spaced times on ``[0, horizon]``. |
| `VarianceExplodingSDE` | class | Variance-exploding SDE ``dx = sqrt(d sigma^2(t) / dt) dw``. |
| `VariancePreservingSDE` | class | Variance-preserving SDE ``dx = -beta(t) x / 2 dt + sqrt(beta(t)) dw``. |
| `VectorSamplePath` | class | One realization of a vector-valued process on a time grid. |
| `weak_convergence_study` | function | Estimate the weak error ``\|E f(X_N) - E f(X(T))\|``. |

### `probstat.information` · experimental

Information theory: entropy, divergence, and mutual information.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `alpha_divergence` | function | Return the alpha-divergence ``D_A^(alpha)(p \|\| q)`` for PMFs. |
| `alpha_divergence_gaussian` | function | Return the alpha-divergence between Gaussian laws in closed form. |
| `bernoulli_fisher_information` | function | Return ``I(p) = 1/(p (1 - p))`` for ``Bernoulli(p)``. |
| `beta_entropy` | function | Return the ``Beta(a, b)`` entropy. |
| `beta_fisher_information` | function | Return the ``Beta(a, b)`` information in ``(a, b)``. |
| `bhattacharyya_coefficient` | function | Return ``BC(p, q) = sum_i sqrt(p_i q_i)`` in ``[0, 1]``. |
| `bhattacharyya_distance` | function | Return ``D_B(p, q) = -log BC(p, q)`` (``inf`` for disjoint supports). |
| `bhattacharyya_distance_gaussian` | function | Return the Bhattacharyya distance between Gaussian laws. |
| `binomial_fisher_information` | function | Return ``I(p) = n/(p (1 - p))`` for ``Binomial(n, p)`` with ``n`` known. |
| `bures_distance` | function | Return the Bures distance between positive-semidefinite matrices. |
| `categorical_fisher_information` | function | Return the categorical information in the first ``k - 1`` probabilities. |
| `conditional_entropy` | function | Return ``H(Y \| X) = -sum p(x, y) log(p(x, y)/p(x))``. |
| `cross_entropy` | function | Return the cross-entropy ``H(p, q) = -sum p log q = H(p) + D(p \|\| q)``. |
| `differential_entropy` | function | Return the closed-form differential entropy of a supported law. |
| `discrete_wasserstein` | function | Return ``W_p = (min_P sum_ij \|x_i - y_j\|^p P_ij)^(1/p)`` exactly. |
| `empirical_wasserstein_1d` | function | Return ``W_p`` between two discrete laws on the line, exactly. |
| `entropy` | function | Return the Shannon entropy ``H(X) = -sum p log p``. |
| `exact_transport` | function | Solve the discrete Kantorovich problem exactly by the transportation simplex. |
| `exponential_entropy` | function | Return ``h = 1 - log lambda`` for ``Exponential(lambda)``. |
| `exponential_fisher_information` | function | Return ``I(lambda) = 1/lambda^2`` for ``Exponential(lambda)``. |
| `fisher_information` | function | Return the closed-form Fisher information of a supported law. |
| `fisher_rao_bernoulli` | function | Return ``2 \|asin sqrt(p0) - asin sqrt(p1)\|`` between Bernoulli laws. |
| `fisher_rao_binomial` | function | Return ``2 sqrt(n) \|asin sqrt(p0) - asin sqrt(p1)\|`` (``n`` known). |
| `fisher_rao_categorical` | function | Return ``2 arccos(sum_i sqrt(p_i q_i))`` between categorical laws. |
| `fisher_rao_exponential` | function | Return ``\|log(lambda0/lambda1)\|`` between exponential laws. |
| `fisher_rao_normal` | function | Return the Fisher-Rao distance between univariate normal laws. |
| `fisher_rao_normal_mean` | function | Return the Mahalanobis distance ``sqrt((m0 - m1)' S^-1 (m0 - m1))``. |
| `fisher_rao_poisson` | function | Return ``2 \|sqrt(lambda0) - sqrt(lambda1)\|`` between Poisson laws. |
| `gamma_entropy` | function | Return ``h = k - log lambda + log Gamma(k) + (1 - k) psi(k)``. |
| `gamma_fisher_information` | function | Return the ``Gamma(k, lambda)`` information in ``(k, lambda)``. |
| `gaussian_barycenter` | function | Return the ``W_2`` barycenter of Gaussian laws by fixed-point iteration. |
| `gaussian_wasserstein` | function | Return ``W_2(N(m0, S0), N(m1, S1)) = sqrt(\|m0 - m1\|^2 + B(S0, S1)^2)``. |
| `GaussianBarycenter` | class | The ``W_2`` barycenter of Gaussian laws. |
| `gev_entropy` | function | Return ``h = log sigma + gamma_E (1 + xi) + 1`` for the GEV law. |
| `hellinger_distance` | function | Return ``H(p, q) = sqrt(sum_i (sqrt p_i - sqrt q_i)^2 / 2)`` in ``[0, 1]``. |
| `hellinger_distance_gaussian` | function | Return ``H = sqrt(1 - exp(-D_B))`` between Gaussian laws. |
| `InformationUnit` | constant |  |
| `jensen_shannon_divergence` | function | Return ``JS(p, q) = D(p \|\| m)/2 + D(q \|\| m)/2`` with ``m = (p + q)/2``. |
| `joint_entropy` | function | Return ``H(X, Y) = -sum p(x, y) log p(x, y)``. |
| `kl_divergence` | function | Return the Kullback-Leibler divergence ``D(p \|\| q) = sum p log(p/q)``. |
| `kl_multivariate_normal` | function | Return ``D(N(m0, S0) \|\| N(m1, S1))`` in closed form. |
| `kl_normal` | function | Return ``D(N0 \|\| N1)`` for univariate normal laws. |
| `kozachenko_leonenko_entropy` | function | Estimate differential entropy by the Kozachenko-Leonenko ``k``-NN rule. |
| `ksg_mutual_information` | function | Estimate ``I(X; Y)`` by the Kraskov-Stoegbauer-Grassberger estimator. |
| `lognormal_entropy` | function | Return ``h = mu + log(sigma sqrt(2 pi e))`` when ``log X ~ N(mu, sigma^2)``. |
| `marginals` | function | Return the marginal PMFs ``(p_X, p_Y)`` of a joint table. |
| `multivariate_normal_entropy` | function | Return ``h = (d log(2 pi e) + log det Sigma)/2`` for a ``d``-variate normal. |
| `multivariate_normal_mean_fisher_information` | function | Return ``I(m) = S^(-1)`` for the mean of ``N(m, S)`` with ``S`` known. |
| `mutual_information` | function | Return ``I(X; Y) = sum p(x, y) log(p(x, y)/(p(x) p(y)))``. |
| `normal_entropy` | function | Return ``h = log(sigma sqrt(2 pi e))`` for ``Normal(mu, sigma^2)``. |
| `normal_fisher_information` | function | Return ``I(mu, sigma) = diag(1/sigma^2, 2/sigma^2)`` for ``N(mu, sigma^2)``. |
| `normal_wasserstein` | function | Return ``W_2 = sqrt((mu0 - mu1)^2 + (sigma0 - sigma1)^2)`` for two normals. |
| `numerical_fisher_information` | function | Return a numerical Fisher information for any distribution family. |
| `OptimalityCertificate` | class | Primal-dual optimality evidence for a transport plan. |
| `poisson_fisher_information` | function | Return ``I(lambda) = 1/lambda`` for ``Poisson(lambda)``. |
| `power_cost_matrix` | function | Return ``C_ij = \|x_i - y_j\|^p`` with the Euclidean norm. |
| `quantile_barycenter` | function | Return the ``W_2`` barycenter of 1-D laws by averaging quantiles. |
| `quantile_wasserstein` | function | Return ``W_p`` between 1-D laws from their quantile functions. |
| `QuantileBarycenter` | class | The ``W_2`` barycenter of 1-D laws, ``Q(u) = sum_k w_k Q_k(u)``. |
| `renyi_divergence` | function | Return the Renyi divergence ``D_alpha(p \|\| q)`` of order ``alpha``. |
| `renyi_divergence_gaussian` | function | Return ``D_alpha(N(m0, S0) \|\| N(m1, S1))`` in closed form. |
| `sinkhorn` | function | Solve entropic optimal transport by log-domain Sinkhorn iterations. |
| `sinkhorn_divergence` | function | Return the debiased Sinkhorn divergence between two point clouds. |
| `SinkhornResult` | class | Output of log-domain Sinkhorn iterations. |
| `sliced_wasserstein` | function | Estimate the sliced Wasserstein distance between two point clouds. |
| `SlicedWassersteinResult` | class | Monte Carlo estimate of a sliced Wasserstein distance. |
| `squared_euclidean_cost_matrix` | function | Return ``C_ij = \|x_i - y_j\|^2``, the cost behind ``W_2``. |
| `total_variation_distance` | function | Return ``TV(p, q) = sum_i \|p_i - q_i\| / 2 = max_A \|P(A) - Q(A)\|``. |
| `total_variation_gaussian_shared_covariance` | function | Return ``TV = 2 Phi(Delta/2) - 1 = erf(Delta / (2 sqrt 2))``. |
| `total_variation_normal` | function | Return the exact total variation between two univariate normal laws. |
| `transport_certificate` | function | Check a plan and dual potentials for optimality. |
| `TransportPlan` | class | An exact optimal transport plan with dual potentials. |
| `unbalanced_sinkhorn` | function | Solve unbalanced entropic transport by damped log-domain Sinkhorn. |
| `UnbalancedSinkhornResult` | class | Output of the unbalanced Sinkhorn (scaling) iterations. |
| `uniform_entropy` | function | Return ``h = log(b - a)`` for ``Uniform(a, b)``. |
| `UNITS` | constant |  |

## Inference and modelling

Estimation, Bayesian computation, regression, survival, time series, causal effects, conformal prediction, experimental design, and generative models (normalizing flows, energy-based models).

### `probstat.inference` · provisional

Statistical estimation, testing, resampling, Bayesian updating, and diagnostics.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `abc_rejection` | function | Sample the ABC posterior by rejection. |
| `abc_smc` | function | Sample the ABC posterior by population Monte Carlo (ABC-PMC). |
| `ABCResult` | class | Accepted parameters, weights, and costs of an ABC run. |
| `adaptation_windows` | function | Return the simplified Stan-style warm-up schedule. |
| `adaptive_metropolis` | function | Sample by adaptive Metropolis with adaptation confined to warm-up. |
| `AdditiveModelFit` | class | A fitted Gaussian additive model. |
| `AdditiveTerm` | class | One smooth term ``f_j(x) = sum_k beta_jk B_jk(x)`` of an additive model. |
| `adjust_p_values` | function | Adjust a family of p-values for multiple testing. |
| `advi` | function | Fit a Gaussian approximation by reparameterization-gradient ADVI. |
| `akaike_weights` | function | Return normalized weights ``exp(-Delta_i / 2) / sum_j exp(-Delta_j / 2)``. |
| `AmortizedGaussianPosterior` | class | A trained Gaussian neural posterior ``q(theta \| x)``. |
| `anderson_darling_asymptotic_cdf` | function | Evaluate the limiting null CDF ``ADinf(z) = lim P(A_n^2 < z)``. |
| `anderson_darling_cdf` | function | Evaluate the finite-sample null CDF ``P(A_n^2 < z)``. |
| `anderson_darling_test` | function | Compute the one-sample Anderson-Darling statistic and its p-value. |
| `AndersonDarlingResult` | class | One-sample Anderson-Darling statistic for a fully specified CDF. |
| `AnovaResult` | class | One-way analysis-of-variance F test. |
| `AnovaRow` | class | One term of an ANOVA table. |
| `AnovaTable` | class | ANOVA table of a linear model. |
| `backfit_additive_model` | function | Fit a Gaussian additive model by backfitting with fixed smoothing. |
| `bartlett_test` | function | Run Bartlett's test of equal variances for normal groups. |
| `beta_binomial_log_likelihood` | function | Return the beta-binomial marginal log-likelihood. |
| `BETA_FAMILY` | constant |  |
| `BetaBinomialEmpiricalBayes` | class | Type-II maximum-likelihood fit of a beta-binomial hierarchy. |
| `betting_confidence_sequence` | function | Compute a confidence sequence for a bounded mean by betting. |
| `betting_mean_test` | function | Test the mean of bounded observations sequentially by betting. |

### `probstat.inference.ppl` · provisional

A small probabilistic-programming interface over the inference engines.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `Bijection` | class | Smooth bijection from ``R`` onto the interior of an interval support. |
| `bijection_for` | function | Choose the bijection to unconstrained space for an interval support. |
| `condition` | function | Return a model in which the named sites are observed with ``data``. |
| `DiscreteDraws` | class | Recovered posterior draws and conditional probabilities of discrete sites. |
| `elementwise` | function | Build one distribution per element by broadcasting arguments. |
| `fit_advi` | function | Fit a Gaussian ADVI approximation on the unconstrained space. |
| `laplace` | function | Approximate the posterior by a Gaussian at the unconstrained mode. |
| `LogDensityTerms` | class | Decomposition of the joint log-density at one unconstrained point. |
| `ModelHandle` | class | The object a model function receives as its argument ``m``. |
| `ModelIR` | class | Traced intermediate representation of a model. |
| `ModelSpecificationError` | class | A model violates the rules of the probabilistic-programming interface. |
| `OrderedVector` | class | The law of the sorted vector of ``size`` i.i.d. draws from ``base``. |
| `posterior_predictive` | function | Simulate replicated data from the posterior predictive distribution. |
| `PosteriorSamples` | class | Posterior draws of a probabilistic program, keyed by site name. |
| `predictive_pvalues` | function | Run :func:`posterior_predictive_pvalues` for one observed site. |
| `PredictiveSamples` | class | Simulated values of every site of a model, keyed by site name. |
| `prior_predictive` | function | Simulate every site from the prior predictive distribution. |
| `PseudoPrior` | class | An independent normal pseudo-prior on a site's unconstrained coordinates. |
| `sample` | function | Draw posterior samples of a probabilistic program by MCMC. |
| `SiteInfo` | class | One named site of a traced model. |
| `StructuredSupport` | class | A structured support and its bijection from unconstrained space. |
| `trace` | function | Execute a model and return its intermediate representation. |
| `TracedStructure` | class | The sites one configuration of the global discrete sites declares. |
| `TruncatedDiscrete` | class | A discrete law restricted to ``{lower, ..., upper}`` and renormalized. |
| `tune_pseudo_priors` | function | Moment-match normal pseudo-priors to a pilot run's conditional draws. |

### `probstat.inference.survival` · provisional

Survival analysis for right-censored time-to-event data.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `compare_restricted_means` | function | Compare the restricted mean survival times of two independent groups. |
| `cox_partial_likelihood` | function | Evaluate the Cox partial log-likelihood with its derivatives. |
| `CoxModelResult` | class | Fitted Cox proportional-hazards model with Wald inference. |
| `cumulative_incidence` | function | Estimate the cumulative incidence of one cause by Aalen-Johansen. |
| `cumulative_incidence_curves` | function | Estimate the cumulative incidence of every observed cause. |
| `CumulativeIncidenceEstimate` | class | Aalen-Johansen cumulative incidence of one cause with its variance. |
| `fit_cox_model` | function | Fit a Cox proportional-hazards model by Newton-Raphson. |
| `fit_parametric_survival` | function | Fit an exponential, Weibull, or lognormal AFT model to censored data. |
| `kaplan_meier` | function | Estimate the survival function by the Kaplan-Meier product limit. |
| `KaplanMeierEstimate` | class | Kaplan-Meier product-limit curve with Greenwood variance and intervals. |
| `log_rank_test` | function | Test equality of ``k >= 2`` survival curves by the log-rank statistic. |
| `LogRankResult` | class | The ``k``-sample log-rank (Mantel-Haenszel) test. |
| `nelson_aalen` | function | Estimate the cumulative hazard by the Nelson-Aalen estimator. |
| `NelsonAalenEstimate` | class | Nelson-Aalen cumulative hazard with its variance and intervals. |
| `ParametricSurvivalFit` | class | Maximum-likelihood AFT fit with observed-information standard errors. |
| `PartialLikelihood` | class | Value, score, and observed information of a Cox partial likelihood. |
| `restricted_mean_survival` | function | Estimate ``E[min(T, tau)]`` by the area under the Kaplan-Meier curve. |
| `RestrictedMeanComparison` | class | Difference and ratio of two restricted mean survival times. |
| `RestrictedMeanSurvival` | class | Restricted mean survival time ``E[min(T, tau)]`` with its interval. |
| `RiskSetRow` | class | Counts at one distinct observed time. |
| `survival_data` | function | Build validated right-censored survival data. |
| `SurvivalData` | class | A right-censored sample ``(t_i, delta_i)``. |
| `SurvivalQuantile` | class | A Kaplan-Meier survival quantile with a confidence interval. |

### `probstat.timeseries` · experimental

Time series: correlograms, ARIMA, smoothing, GARCH, VAR, unit roots, change points.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `acf` | function | Return the sample autocorrelations ``rho_hat(0..max_lag)``. |
| `adf_critical_values` | function | Return MacKinnon (2010) Dickey-Fuller critical values for ``T`` observations. |
| `adf_simulated_p_value` | function | Return the simulated Dickey-Fuller p-value and its Monte Carlo error. |
| `adf_test` | function | Run the augmented Dickey-Fuller test for a unit root. |
| `ADFResult` | class | Augmented Dickey-Fuller test result. |
| `ar_root_check` | function | Check causality (stationarity) of ``phi(z) = 1 - sum phi_j z^j``. |
| `arch_lm_test` | function | Return Engle's LM test for autoregressive conditional heteroskedasticity. |
| `ARCHTestResult` | class | Engle's Lagrange-multiplier test for ARCH effects. |
| `ARIMAFit` | class | Maximum-likelihood ARIMA(p, d, q) fit. |
| `ARIMAForecast` | class | h-step forecasts with exact Gaussian prediction intervals. |
| `arma_autocovariance` | function | Return the theoretical autocovariances ``gamma(0..max_lag)`` of an ARMA. |
| `arma_log_likelihood` | function | Return the exact Gaussian log-likelihood of a stationary ARMA(p, q). |
| `arma_state_space` | function | Build the Harvey state-space form of a stationary ARMA(p, q) model. |
| `ARMAStateSpace` | class | Harvey state-space form of a stationary Gaussian ARMA(p, q) process. |
| `autocovariance` | function | Return the sample autocovariances ``gamma_hat(0..max_lag)``. |
| `bartlett_band` | function | Return Bartlett half-widths for testing an MA(k-1) null at each lag ``k``. |
| `bayesian_online_changepoint` | function | Run the Adams-MacKay run-length recursion with a constant hazard. |
| `centered_moving_average` | function | Return the centred moving average that removes a period-``s`` cycle. |
| `ChangePointResult` | class | Optimal segmentation under a penalized Gaussian cost. |
| `classical_decomposition` | function | Decompose a series by the classical moving-average method. |
| `ClassicalDecomposition` | class | Moving-average decomposition into trend, seasonal, and remainder parts. |
| `coefficients_to_pacf` | function | Map AR coefficients to reflection (partial autocorrelation) coefficients. |
| `cusum_test` | function | Test for a single change in the mean with the CUSUM of deviations. |
| `CUSUMResult` | class | CUSUM test for a single shift in the mean. |
| `difference` | function | Apply ``(1 - B^lag)^order`` to a series. |
| `durbin_levinson` | function | Run the Durbin-Levinson recursion on an autocorrelation sequence. |
| `DurbinLevinsonResult` | class | All Yule-Walker solutions of orders ``1..K`` from one autocorrelation run. |
| `ExponentialSmoothingFit` | class | Fitted simple or Holt exponential smoothing model. |
| `fit_arima` | function | Fit an ARIMA(p, d, q) model by exact Gaussian maximum likelihood. |
| `fit_garch` | function | Fit a GARCH(p, q) model by conditional maximum likelihood. |
| `fit_var` | function | Fit a VAR(p) by multivariate least squares. |
| `forecast_arima` | function | Forecast a fitted ARIMA model with exact finite-sample intervals. |
| `GammaPoissonPrior` | class | Conjugate Gamma prior for the rate of Poisson counts. |
| `garch_log_likelihood` | function | Return the conditional GARCH log-likelihood of a series. |
| `garch_variances` | function | Return the GARCH conditional variances ``sigma_1^2..sigma_n^2``. |
| `GARCHDiagnostics` | class | Checks on the standardized residuals ``z_t = e_t / sigma_t``. |
| `GARCHFit` | class | Maximum-likelihood GARCH(p, q) fit. |
| `GARCHForecast` | class | Multi-step GARCH variance forecasts. |
| `GARCHSimulation` | class | A simulated GARCH path. |
| `GrangerCausalityResult` | class | Wald and F tests that some variables do not Granger-cause others. |
| `hierarchy_summing_matrix` | function | Build the summing matrix from a parent-to-children specification. |
| `HierarchyStructure` | class | Series names and the summing matrix of an aggregation structure. |
| `holt_linear` | function | Fit Holt's linear-trend exponential smoothing with optimized parameters. |
| `ImpulseResponseBands` | class | Pointwise bootstrap bands for VAR impulse responses. |
| `is_invertible` | function | Return whether an MA polynomial is invertible (all roots outside \|z\| = 1). |
| `is_stationary` | function | Return whether an AR polynomial is causal (all roots outside \|z\| = 1). |
| `kolmogorov_sf` | function | Return ``P(sup_t \|B(t)\| > b)`` for a Brownian bridge ``B``. |
| `kpss_asymptotic_sf` | function | Return ``P(eta > x)`` under the limiting null law of the KPSS statistic. |
| `kpss_critical_values` | function | Return upper-tail critical values of the KPSS limiting null law. |
| `kpss_test` | function | Run the KPSS test of the null of level or trend stationarity. |
| `KPSSResult` | class | KPSS stationarity test result. |
| `ljung_box` | function | Return the Ljung-Box statistic and its asymptotic chi-square p-value. |
| `LjungBoxResult` | class | Ljung-Box portmanteau test of no autocorrelation up to lag ``h``. |
| `ma_root_check` | function | Check invertibility of ``theta(z) = 1 + sum theta_j z^j``. |
| `newey_west_variance` | function | Return the Bartlett-kernel (Newey-West) long-run variance. |
| `NormalInverseGammaPrior` | class | Conjugate prior for Gaussian data with unknown mean and variance. |
| `OnlineChangePointResult` | class | Run-length filtering distributions of a series. |
| `pacf` | function | Return the sample partial autocorrelations ``phi_hat_kk`` for ``k = 1..K``. |
| `pacf_to_coefficients` | function | Map reflection coefficients in ``(-1, 1)`` to stationary AR coefficients. |
| `pelt` | function | Find multiple change points by PELT with a penalized Gaussian cost. |
| `polynomial_roots` | function | Return all complex roots of ``c_0 + c_1 z + ... + c_n z^n``. |
| `psi_weights` | function | Return the MA(infinity) weights ``psi_0..psi_(count-1)`` of an ARMA model. |
| `reconcile_forecasts` | function | Reconcile base forecasts so that they satisfy the aggregation constraints. |
| `ReconciliationResult` | class | Reconciled forecasts and the linear map that produced them. |
| `ResidualDiagnostics` | class | Residual checks for a fitted ARIMA model. |
| `robust_noise_variance` | function | Estimate the noise variance from first differences by the MAD. |
| `RootCheck` | class | Root-based verdict for one lag polynomial. |
| `seasonal_difference` | function | Apply the seasonal difference ``(1 - B^s)^D``. |
| `Segment` | class | One segment of a segmentation. |
| `select_var_order` | function | Compare VAR orders ``0..max_order`` by AIC, BIC, HQ, and FPE. |
| `shrink_covariance` | function | Shrink the sample covariance of forecast errors towards its diagonal. |
| `ShrunkCovariance` | class | Sample covariance shrunk towards its diagonal. |
| `simple_exponential_smoothing` | function | Fit simple exponential smoothing with an optimized ``alpha``. |
| `simulate_arima` | function | Simulate an ARIMA(p, d, q) path by integrating a simulated ARMA path. |
| `simulate_arma` | function | Simulate a stationary Gaussian ARMA(p, q) path with a burn-in period. |
| `simulate_garch` | function | Simulate a stationary GARCH(p, q) path with a burn-in. |
| `simulate_var` | function | Simulate a Gaussian VAR(p) path with a burn-in. |
| `undifference` | function | Invert ordinary differencing given the first ``d`` original observations. |
| `VARFit` | class | Least-squares VAR(p) fit. |
| `VARForecast` | class | Point forecasts and Gaussian intervals of a VAR. |
| `VAROrderSelection` | class | Information criteria for VAR orders ``0..max_order`` on one sample. |

### `probstat.causal` · experimental

Causal graphs, structural causal models, effect estimation, and sensitivity.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|

| `AdjustmentComparison` | class | Naive conditional versus backdoor-adjusted outcome probabilities. |
| `AggregatedEffect` | class | A weighted average of group-time effects. |
| `backdoor_adjustment` | function | Return ``P(y \| do(x)) = sum_z P(y \| x, z) P(z)`` from observational data. |
| `BalanceTable` | class | Standardized mean differences for every covariate. |
| `bootstrap_effect` | function | Bootstrap an ATE estimator by resampling whole units with replacement. |
| `CausalGraph` | class | An immutable, validated causal directed acyclic graph. |
| `compare_naive_and_adjusted` | function | Contrast naive conditioning with backdoor adjustment for one outcome. |
| `ConfounderAdjustment` | class | Regression results adjusted for a hypothesized omitted confounder. |
| `confounding_bias_factor` | function | Return the maximal bias factor of an unmeasured confounder. |
| `CovariateBalance` | class | Balance summary of one covariate. |
| `CyclicGraphError` | class | Raised when the supplied edges contain a directed cycle. |
| `did_two_by_two` | function | Estimate the 2x2 difference-in-differences from unit-level data. |
| `DifferenceInDifferencesResult` | class | Canonical 2x2 difference-in-differences estimate. |
| `DiscreteCausalModel` | class | A causal Bayesian network on finite variables. |
| `dml_average_treatment_effect` | function | Estimate the ATE of a binary treatment by cross-fitted AIPW. |
| `dml_partially_linear` | function | Estimate ``theta`` in ``Y = theta D + g(X) + e`` by double ML. |
| `DoubleMLResult` | class | Cross-fitted estimate of ``theta`` in the partially linear model. |
| `e_value` | function | Compute the E-value of a risk ratio and, optionally, of its interval. |
| `EffectEstimate` | class | A point estimate of the average treatment effect and its context. |
| `estimate_aipw` | function | Estimate the ATE by augmented inverse-probability weighting. |
| `estimate_ipw` | function | Estimate the ATE by inverse-probability weighting. |
| `estimate_regression_adjustment` | function | Estimate the ATE by standardization over per-arm linear outcome models. |
| `EValue` | class | E-values for a risk-ratio point estimate and its confidence limit. |
| `fit_propensity_scores` | function | Fit ``logit e(x) = b0 + x'b`` by maximum likelihood (IRLS). |
| `frontdoor_adjustment` | function | Return ``sum_m P(m \| x) sum_x' P(y \| x', m) P(x')`` from observational data. |
| `group_time_effects` | function | Estimate every ``ATT(g, t)``, ``t >= g``, of a staggered design. |
| `GroupTimeEffect` | class | One group-time average treatment effect ``ATT(g, t)``. |
| `GroupTimeEffects` | class | All estimable ``ATT(g, t)`` of a staggered design and their averages. |
| `InstrumentalVariablesResult` | class | Two-stage least-squares fit. |
| `JointTable` | class | A probability mass function over all assignments of finite variables. |
| `knn_learner` | function | Return a k-nearest-neighbour regression learner. |
| `linear_learner` | function | Return a learner that fits least squares with an intercept. |
| `logistic_learner` | function | Return a learner that fits a logistic regression with an intercept. |
| `minimal_adjustment_sets` | function | Enumerate every minimal observed set satisfying the backdoor criterion. |
| `omitted_variable_bias` | function | Return the absolute bias caused by omitting a confounder. |
| `panel_from_long` | function | Reshape long-format panel records into a balanced unit-by-period matrix. |
| `partial_r2` | function | Return the partial R-squared of a regressor from its t statistic. |
| `PlaceboInference` | class | In-space placebo comparison for a synthetic control. |
| `PositivityWarning` | class | Emitted when propensity scores come close to zero or one. |
| `project_to_simplex` | function | Project a vector onto the probability simplex in Euclidean norm. |
| `PropensityModel` | class | Logistic-regression propensity scores ``e(x) = P(T = 1 \| x)``. |
| `ridge_learner` | function | Return a learner that fits ridge regression with a fixed penalty. |
| `risk_ratio_from_odds_ratio` | function | Approximate a risk ratio from an odds ratio for E-value purposes. |
| `risk_ratio_from_standardized_difference` | function | Approximate a risk ratio from a standardized mean difference ``d``. |
| `robustness_value` | function | Return the robustness value of a regression coefficient. |
| `satisfies_backdoor` | function | Test the backdoor criterion for an adjustment set. |
| `satisfies_frontdoor` | function | Test the frontdoor criterion for a mediator set. |
| `sensitivity_statistics` | function | Summarize how fragile a regression coefficient is to a confounder. |

| `standardized_mean_differences` | function | Compute standardized mean differences between treatment arms. |
| `synthetic_control` | function | Fit a synthetic control and return the effect path. |
| `synthetic_control_placebo` | function | Rank the treated unit's RMSPE ratio among in-space placebos. |
| `synthetic_control_weights` | function | Find the convex donor combination closest to the treated unit. |
