### `probstat.conformal` · experimental

Distribution-free conformal prediction intervals for any point predictor.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `adaptive_conformal_intervals` | function | Run adaptive conformal inference over a prediction/response sequence. |
| `AdaptiveConformalPredictor` | class | Online adaptive conformal intervals ``yhat_t +/- q_t s_t``. |
| `AdaptiveConformalResult` | class | Intervals, levels, and miss indicators of one ACI pass over a sequence. |
| `as_predictor` | function | Return a plain callable for a callable or a ``predict``-bearing object. |
| `conformal_outlier_detection` | function | Flag outliers by Benjamini-Hochberg on conformal p-values. |
| `conformal_p_values` | function | Return ``(1 + #{i : s_i >= s_j}) / (n + 1)`` for each test score. |
| `conformal_quantile` | function | Return the finite-sample conformal quantile of calibration scores. |
| `conformal_rank` | function | Return the conformal order-statistic index ``k = ceil((1-alpha)(n+1))``. |
| `conformal_risk_control` | function | Choose the smallest grid ``lambda`` whose adjusted risk is at most ``alpha``. |
| `conformalize_surrogate` | function | Calibrate conformal error bars for a surrogate of one model QoI. |
| `conformalized_quantile_regression` | function | Calibrate given lower/upper quantile predictors on held-out data. |
| `ConformalizedQuantileRegressor` | class | Conformalized quantile regression ``[q_lo(x) - q_hat, q_hi(x) + q_hat]``. |
| `ConformalOutlierResult` | class | Conformal p-values and Benjamini-Hochberg outlier decisions. |
| `ConformalRiskControlResult` | class | Calibrated parameter and risk curve of conformal risk control. |
| `CoverageReport` | class | Empirical coverage of prediction intervals on a test set. |
| `cv_plus_regression` | function | Build CV+ intervals from ``K`` model fits on K-fold splits. |
| `CVPlusRegressor` | class | CV+ intervals from ``K`` fold predictors and ``n`` out-of-fold residuals. |
| `empirical_coverage` | function | Measure the empirical coverage of intervals on test responses. |
| `jackknife_plus_regression` | function | Build jackknife+ intervals by refitting the model ``n`` times. |
| `JackknifePlusRegressor` | class | Jackknife+ intervals from leave-one-out predictors and residuals. |
| `lower_conformal_rank` | function | Return ``floor(alpha (n + 1))``, the lower jackknife+ order index. |
| `mondrian_conformal_regression` | function | Calibrate one split conformal quantile per group label. |
| `MondrianConformalRegressor` | class | Group-conditional split conformal intervals ``mu(x) +/- q_g s(x)``. |
| `MondrianGroup` | class | Calibration summary of one group. |
| `nonexchangeable_conformal_quantile` | function | Return the conformal quantile for fixed weights ``w_i`` in ``[0, 1]``. |
| `nonexchangeable_coverage_gap` | function | Return ``sum_i w~_i d_i`` for user-supplied total-variation bounds. |
| `nonexchangeable_split_conformal_regression` | function | Calibrate split conformal intervals with fixed weights in ``[0, 1]``. |
| `online_quantile_intervals` | function | Run quantile-tracking online conformal prediction over a sequence. |
| `OnlineQuantileResult` | class | Intervals, thresholds, and miss indicators of one quantile-tracking pass. |
| `OnlineQuantileTracker` | class | Online intervals ``yhat_t +/- q_t s_t`` with a tracked threshold ``q_t``. |
| `PredictionInterval` | class | A prediction interval ``[lower, upper]`` and the point it was built on. |
| `split_conformal_coverage` | function | Return the marginal coverage ``k / (n + 1)`` of split conformal sets. |
| `split_conformal_regression` | function | Calibrate split conformal regression intervals on held-out data. |
| `SplitConformalRegressor` | class | Split conformal intervals ``mu(x) +/- q_hat s(x)`` around a predictor. |
| `SurrogateConformalResult` | class | Conformal calibration of a surrogate against its true model. |
| `VarianceSurrogate` | class | A surrogate that also reports a predictive variance (e.g. a GP). |
| `weighted_conformal_quantile` | function | Return the weighted conformal quantile with mass at ``+inf``. |
| `weighted_split_conformal_regression` | function | Calibrate split conformal intervals for a test law with shifted inputs. |
| `WeightedConformalRegressor` | class | Weighted split conformal intervals ``mu(x) +/- q(x) s(x)``. |

Optimal and Bayesian experimental design.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `ACQUISITIONS` | constant |  |

| `bayesian_optimization` | function | Optimize an expensive function over a box by Bayesian optimization. |

| `confidence_bound` | function | Return the optimistic confidence bound ``mu +/- sqrt(beta) s``. |

| `contrastive_lower_bound_eig` | function | Estimate a lower bound of the EIG by the prior contrastive bound. |

| `estimate_eig` | function | Estimate the EIG of a design by the chosen method. |

Generative models: flows, energy models, flow matching, neural SBI (experimental).

|---|---|---|

| `fit_denoising_score_matching` | function | Fit an energy model by denoising score matching with Gaussian noise. |
| `fit_flow` | function | Fit a normalizing flow by maximum likelihood with Adam mini-batches. |
| `fit_flow_matching` | function | Fit a velocity network to data by conditional flow matching. |
| `fit_flow_matching_posterior` | function | Train a flow-matching posterior on simulated parameter/data pairs. |
| `fit_ratio_estimator` | function | Train a likelihood-to-evidence ratio estimator on simulated pairs. |
| `fit_score_matching` | function | Fit an energy model by implicit score matching with Adam mini-batches. |
| `flow_log_density` | function | Return ``log p_1(x)`` of the law transported from ``N(0, I)``. |
| `flow_matching_loss_and_gradient` | function | Return the flow-matching loss of a fixed batch and its gradient. |
| `FlowFit` | class | Maximum-likelihood fit of a :class:`NormalizingFlow`. |
| `FlowLayer` | class | Structural interface of one invertible flow layer. |
| `FlowMatchingFit` | class | Velocity network fitted by conditional flow matching. |
| `FlowMatchingPosterior` | class | A trained flow-matching posterior ``q(theta \| x)``. |
| `GaussianMarginalVelocity` | class | Exact marginal velocity field for independent Gaussian data. |
| `GridPosterior` | class | One-dimensional posterior tabulated on a grid. |
| `importance_normalizing_constant` | function | Return ``log Z`` from draws of a normalized proposal ``q``. |
| `integrate_flow` | function | Transport a base point by ``dx/dt = v(t, x)`` from ``t = 0`` to ``1``. |
| `kl_divergence_estimate` | function | Return the Monte Carlo estimate of ``KL(p \|\| q)`` from draws of ``p``. |
| `LangevinResult` | class | Output of :func:`sample_langevin`. |
| `mean_log_likelihood` | function | Return the mean log-density of data and its standard error. |
| `NormalizingConstant` | class | Estimate of ``log Z`` for ``Z = int f(x) dx``. |
| `NormalizingFlow` | class | Composition ``x = T_K(... T_1(z))`` of flow layers over ``z ~ N(0, I)``. |
| `Permutation` | class | Fixed coordinate permutation ``x_i = z_{order[i]}`` (volume preserving). |
| `PlanarFlow` | class | Planar flow ``x = z + u_hat tanh(w'z + b)``. |
| `quadrature_normalizing_constant` | function | Return ``log int exp(log f(x)) dx`` over a box by adaptive quadrature. |
| `RadialFlow` | class | Radial flow ``x = z + beta (z - z0) / (alpha + \|z - z0\|)``. |
| `ratio_loss_and_gradient` | function | Return the balanced cross-entropy of a fixed batch and its gradient. |
| `RatioEstimator` | class | A trained estimator of ``log r(theta, x) = log p(x \| theta) / p(x)``. |
| `real_nvp_flow` | function | Return a Real NVP style flow: couplings, reversals, and a final actnorm. |
| `sample_langevin` | function | Sample a density known up to a constant by (adjusted) Langevin steps. |
| `SampleMean` | class | A sample mean with its standard error ``s / sqrt(n)``. |
| `score_matching_objective` | function | Return the implicit score-matching objective on a data set. |
| `VelocityNetwork` | class | Velocity model ``v_theta(t, x, c)`` built on a ``tanh`` MLP. |

## Forward uncertainty quantification

The central probabilistic model, uncertainty taxonomy, propagation, Monte Carlo, sensitivity, and surrogates.

### `probstat.modeling` · provisional

Central probabilistic-model contract shared by every UQ workflow.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `archimedean_theta_from_tau` | function | Invert Kendall's tau for a one-parameter Archimedean family. |
| `ArchimedeanCopula` | class | Exchangeable Archimedean copula ``C(u) = psi(sum_i phi(u_i))``. |
| `ClaytonCopula` | class | Clayton copula: ``psi(t) = (1 + t)^(-1/theta)``, ``theta > 0``. |
| `ContinuousMarginal` | class | Structural interface required of a marginal input law. |
| `Copula` | class | Abstract ``d``-dimensional copula on the unit hypercube. |
| `CopulaFit` | class | A fitted copula with the empirical dependence it was fitted to. |
| `CVineCopula` | class | Canonical vine: tree ``t`` is a star around the ``t``-th root. |
| `DiscreteMarginal` | class | Structural interface of an integer-valued marginal input law. |
| `DVineCopula` | class | Drawable vine: every tree is a path along the vine order. |
| `EvaluationBatch` | class | Outputs of :meth:`ProbabilisticModel.evaluate_many` with bookkeeping. |
| `EvaluationCounter` | class | Mutable count of forward-model runs, failures, and cache hits. |
| `fit_copula` | function | Fit a copula to tabular data from ranks (marginal-free). |
| `fit_gaussian_copula` | function | Estimate a Gaussian-copula correlation matrix from ranks. |
| `fit_joint_input` | function | Fit marginals per column and a Gaussian copula; return a ``JointInput``. |
| `fit_vine_copula` | function | Fit a C- or D-vine copula by sequential maximum likelihood. |
| `flood_cost` | function | Return the annual dike maintenance cost ``C`` (million euros). |
| `FLOOD_INPUT_NAMES` | constant |  |
| `flood_model` | function | Return the flood model with its eight independent input laws. |
| `FLOOD_NOMINAL` | constant |  |
| `FLOOD_NOMINAL_STANDARD_DEVIATIONS` | constant |  |
| `flood_overflow` | function | Return the dike overflow ``S`` (m) of the flood model. |
| `four_branch` | function | Return the four-branch series limit state (failure when ``<= 0``). |
| `four_branch_model` | function | Return the four-branch limit state on two independent standard normals. |
| `FOUR_BRANCH_REFERENCE_PROBABILITY` | constant |  |
| `frank_tau` | function | Return Kendall's tau of the Frank copula with parameter ``theta``. |
| `FrankCopula` | class | Frank copula: ``psi(t) = -log(1 - (1 - e^-theta) e^-t) / theta``. |
| `gauss_hermite` | function | Return probabilists' Gauss-Hermite nodes and weights by Golub-Welsch. |
| `GaussianCopula` | class | Gaussian (Nataf) copula with latent correlation matrix ``R``. |
| `GaussianCopulaFit` | class | Rank-based Gaussian-copula correlation estimate. |
| `GumbelCopula` | class | Gumbel (Gumbel-Hougaard) copula: ``psi(t) = exp(-t^(1/theta))``, ``theta >= 1``. |
| `IndependenceCopula` | class | The independence copula ``C(u) = prod_i u_i``. |
| `IntegerCodedCategorical` | class | A categorical law coded as the integers ``0, ..., K-1``. |
| `IntervalInput` | class | An epistemic quantity known only to lie in ``[lower, upper]``. |
| `is_discrete_law` | function | Return whether a marginal law is discrete (has ``pmf`` and no ``pdf``). |
| `ishigami` | function | Return the Ishigami function ``sin x1 + a sin^2 x2 + b x3^4 sin x1``. |
| `ISHIGAMI_FIRST_ORDER` | constant |  |
| `ishigami_model` | function | Return the Ishigami function with inputs independent uniform on ``[-pi, pi]``. |
| `ISHIGAMI_TOTAL` | constant |  |
| `JointInput` | class | Joint law of named inputs: marginals plus a copula. |
| `JointInputFit` | class | Fitted joint input law with per-column fits and the copula estimate. |
| `karhunen_loeve_inputs` | function | Return KL coefficient inputs and the parameter-to-field map. |
| `kendall_independence_p_value` | function | Return the two-sided p-value of the asymptotic Kendall's tau test. |
| `kendall_tau` | function | Return Kendall's tau-b of two paired samples. |
| `kendall_to_latent_correlation` | function | Map Kendall's tau to the latent correlation of an elliptical copula. |
| `nataf_correlation` | function | Return the latent normal correlation reproducing a Pearson correlation. |
| `nearest_correlation_matrix` | function | Return the nearest correlation matrix by Higham's alternating projections. |
| `NearestCorrelation` | class | Result of a nearest-correlation-matrix repair. |
| `normal_scores` | function | Return van der Waerden normal scores ``Phi^-1(rank_i / (n + 1))``. |
| `oscillator` | function | Return the non-linear oscillator limit state (failure when ``<= 0``). |
| `OSCILLATOR_INPUT_NAMES` | constant |  |
| `oscillator_model` | function | Return the non-linear oscillator with its six independent normal inputs. |
| `OSCILLATOR_REFERENCE_PROBABILITY` | constant |  |
| `PAIR_COPULA_FAMILIES` | constant |  |
| `PairCopula` | class | Bivariate building block of a vine: a base copula and a rotation. |
| `ProbabilisticModel` | class | Joint input law, forward model, and quantities of interest. |
| `pseudo_observations` | function | Return rank pseudo-observations ``rank_ij / (n + 1)`` row by row. |
| `RandomFieldInput` | class | Standard-normal KL coefficients of a field, exposed as model inputs. |
| `RandomInput` | class | One named uncertain model input. |
| `rastrigin` | function | Return the modified Rastrigin limit state (failure when ``<= 0``). |
| `rastrigin_model` | function | Return the modified Rastrigin limit state on two independent standard normals. |
| `RASTRIGIN_REFERENCE_PROBABILITY` | constant |  |
| `spearman_to_latent_correlation` | function | Map a Spearman correlation to the Gaussian-copula latent correlation. |
| `standard_normal_cdf` | function | Return ``Phi(z)`` with full relative accuracy in both tails. |
| `standard_normal_quantile` | function | Return ``Phi^-1(p)`` for ``0 < p < 1``. |
| `StudentTCopula` | class | Student-t copula with correlation ``R`` and ``nu`` degrees of freedom. |
| `VineCopula` | class | Common machinery of the C-vine and D-vine pair-copula constructions. |
| `VineCopulaFit` | class | A sequentially fitted C- or D-vine copula. |
| `VineEdge` | class | One edge of a vine: a pair copula with its conditioned and conditioning sets. |
| `VineEdgeFit` | class | The selected pair copula of one vine edge with its fit statistics. |

## Backward analysis and validation

Calibration, filtering and data assimilation, and model validation.

### `probstat.inverse` · provisional

Observation models and auditable inverse-problem calibration workflows.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `abc_tolerance_study` | function | Run ABC rejection over tolerances and seeds against the exact posterior. |
| `ABCOutcome` | class | Result of an ABC run with explicit failure status. |
| `active_surrogate_posterior` | function | Fit a surrogate of a fine log-posterior and refine it where it matters. |
| `ActiveSurrogateResult` | class | Output of :func:`active_surrogate_posterior`. |
| `adaptive_inflation` | function | Inflate an ensemble by the innovation-based factor. |
| `adjoint_gradient` | function | Gradient of ``1/2 \|\|W (G(x) - y)\|\|^2`` by one forward and one adjoint solve. |
| `AdjointGradient` | class | Misfit, residual and gradient from one forward and one adjoint solve. |
| `approximation_error_cost` | function | Cost an approximation-error setup and compare it with fine-only work. |
| `ApproximationErrorCostReport` | class | Cost of an approximation-error model setup in fine-equivalent units. |
| `ApproximationErrorModel` | class | Fitted Gaussian model of the paired error ``e = G_fine - G_coarse``. |
| `AristoffBangerthForward` | class | The benchmark forward map ``theta -> z`` with exact derivatives. |
| `assess_excitation` | function | Evaluate the Bayesian D-criterion and entry precision of a torque sequence. |
| `averaging_kernel` | function | Return the averaging kernel of a functional and its width measures. |
| `AveragingKernel` | class | Averaging kernel of a functional in function space. |
| `backward_heat_problem` | function | Return the discrete backward heat problem with a closed-form SVD. |
| `banana_target` | function | Return the 2D banana posterior of ``y = (x1, x2 + x1^2) + eps``. |
| `bao_degeneracy_report` | function | Measure which nuisance degeneracies BAO breaks and which it does not. |
| `BaoDegeneracyReport` | class | Whitened nuisance spectra of SN, BAO, and combined samples. |
| `BaoDistanceModel` | class | Simplified BAO observable ``D_V(z) / r_s`` with ``r_s`` a nuisance. |
| `bayesian_flux_posterior` | function | Exact linear-Gaussian posterior of ``q`` under a Matern prior. |
| `beck_linear_map` | function | Return the matrix ``G`` of the linear estimator ``q_hat = G y``. |
| `beck_noise_gain` | function | Return the RMS standard deviation of ``q_hat`` for unit noise. |
| `beck_sequential_estimate` | function | Estimate the flux history by sequential function specification. |
| `BeckEstimate` | class | Result of Beck's sequential function specification. |
| `BenchmarkCase` | class | Catalog entry of an inverse benchmark family. |
| `BenchmarkChain` | class | Result of :func:`metropolis_hastings`. |
| `BiasIdentifiability` | class | Measured identifiability of an orbit-determination design. |
| `bimodal_contract` | function | Return the contract ``theta ~ U(-h, h)``, ``y = theta^2 + N(0, s^2)``. |
| `bimodal_exact_posterior` | function | Return the quadrature posterior ``p(theta \| y) ∝ N(y; theta^2, s^2)``. |
| `BimodalComparison` | class | Structured result of :func:`run_bimodal_comparison`. |
| `BimodalConfig` | class | Predeclared budgets and seeds of :func:`run_bimodal_comparison`. |
| `BinomialLaw` | class | Binomial counts ``y_i ~ Bin(n_i, p_i)`` with ``p = expit(eta)``. |
| `bootstrap_lr_calibration` | function | Calibrate the profile likelihood-ratio threshold by parametric bootstrap. |
| `BootstrapCalibrationResult` | class | Bootstrap distribution of the profile likelihood-ratio statistic. |
| `BoundaryEffect` | class | Measured effect of the boundary on a discrete Matern prior. |
| `bounded_gauss_newton` | function | Minimize ``1/2 \|\|W (G(x) - y)\|\|^2`` over the box ``l <= x <= u``. |
| `bounded_least_squares` | function | Minimize ``Phi(x)`` of [EQ-INV-LIN-001] on the box ``l <= x <= u``. |

## Reliability and risk

Structural reliability, exact system reliability (BDDs, importance, common-cause failures, phased missions), risk measures, fault trees, and extremes.

### `probstat.reliability` · provisional

Reliability, failure-probability, FORM/SORM, rare-event, and system analysis.

### `probstat.systems` · experimental

Exact system reliability: structures, BDDs, importance, CCF, phased missions.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `AlphaFactorGroup` | class | Alpha-factor CCF model. |

| `ccf_parameter_uncertainty` | function | Propagate uncertain CCF parameters to the system reliability. |

| `CCFIntervalBounds` | class | Unreliability bounds and probability box over interval CCF parameters. |
| `CommonCauseExpansion` | class | A structure whose group members are replaced by their basic events. |
| `compile_expression` | function | Compile a structure expression into a BDD node (``1`` = works). |
| `Component` | class | A named component; ``x = 1`` means the component works. |
| `ComponentModel` | class | Time-dependent reliability and availability of one component. |
| `DemandComponent` | class | A component that fails on demand with a fixed probability. |
| `DFTMonteCarloEstimate` | class | Monte Carlo estimate of the unreliability at one time. |
| `DFTStateSpace` | class | The generated Markov chain of a dynamic fault tree. |

| `DynamicGate` | class | A static or dynamic fault-tree gate. |
| `EpistemicCCFResult` | class | Epistemic mean, variance and variance shares of the system reliability. |
| `expand_common_cause` | function | Replace every group member by the series of its basic events. |
| `ExponentialComponent` | class | A non-repairable component with constant failure rate ``lambda``. |
| `Expression` | constant |  |
| `expression_components` | function | Return component names by first appearance in a left-to-right DFS. |
| `fdep_gate` | function | Return a functional dependency: the trigger fails the dependents. |

### `probstat.risk` · provisional

Quantitative risk analysis: scenarios x probabilities x consequences.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `action_values` | function | Return ``Q[s][a] = r_a(s) + gamma sum_s' P_a(s, s') V(s')``. |
| `and_gate` | function | Return a gate that fails when every input fails. |
| `annual_failure_frequency` | function | Integrate a fragility against a hazard curve (risk convolution). |
| `assess_risk` | function | Propagate a probabilistic model and summarize the risk of one QoI. |
| `backward_induction` | function | Solve the finite-horizon problem by backward induction. |
| `BanditRun` | class | Actions, rewards, and Beta posteriors of one Bernoulli-bandit run. |
| `BasicEvent` | class | An independent basic (primary) failure event. |
| `bellman_residual` | function | Return ``\|T V - V\|_inf`` for the Bellman optimality operator ``T``. |
| `bernoulli_bandit` | function | Run Thompson sampling (or the greedy rule) on a Bernoulli bandit. |
| `binary_heading` | function | Return a two-branch heading with ``success`` and ``failure`` labels. |
| `block_maxima` | function | Return maxima of consecutive, non-overlapping blocks. |
| `bootstrap_risk_interval` | function | Return a seeded nonparametric percentile bootstrap interval. |
| `BootstrapInterval` | class | Percentile bootstrap interval for one sample risk measure. |
| `Branch` | class | One conditional branch leaving a heading. |
| `conditional_value_at_risk` | function | Return the conditional value-at-risk (expected shortfall) of losses. |
| `CriticalityMatrix` | class | Counts and members of a severity-by-criticality grid. |
| `custom_loss` | function | Wrap an arbitrary callable as a validated loss function. |
| `DecisionProblem` | class | Finite decision problem with uncertain states. |
| `decluster_runs` | function | Group exceedances into clusters by the runs method and keep the peaks. |
| `DeclusteredExceedances` | class | Cluster peaks of threshold exceedances found by the runs method. |
| `direct_method` | function | Estimate ``V(pi)`` by ``mean(sum_a pi(a \| x_i) q(x_i, a))``. |
| `DiscreteImpact` | class | A finite discrete impact law. |
| `doubly_robust` | function | Estimate ``V(pi)`` by the direct method plus a weighted residual. |
| `EventSequence` | class | One accident sequence from initiating event to outcome. |
| `EventTree` | class | An initiating event followed by a tree of headings and outcomes. |
| `exceedance_probability` | function | Return the (weighted) empirical probability ``P(L > level)``. |
| `exceedance_rate` | function | Return the exceedance-rate model for observations above ``u``. |
| `ExceedanceRate` | class | Binomial/Poisson model for the number of threshold exceedances. |
| `expected_loss` | function | Return the (weighted) mean loss ``E[L]``. |
| `expected_shortfall` | function | Return expected shortfall, identical to :func:`conditional_value_at_risk`. |
| `ExposureDistribution` | class | Exact finite distribution of the total exposure. |
| `FailureMode` | class | One failure mode of one item, with criticality and optional RPN ranks. |
| `FaultTree` | class | A static coherent fault tree with independent basic events. |
| `FiniteHorizonSolution` | class | Stage-dependent values and decisions from backward induction. |
| `FiniteMDP` | class | Validated finite discounted Markov decision process. |
| `fit_gev` | function | Fit a GEV law to block maxima by maximum likelihood. |
| `fit_gpd` | function | Fit a GPD to non-negative excesses by MLE or by PWM/L-moments. |
| `fit_lognormal_fragility` | function | Fit ``theta`` and ``beta`` by maximum likelihood (Baker 2015). |
| `fit_pot` | function | Fit a peaks-over-threshold model to a series. |
| `Fmeca` | class | A FMECA worksheet: failure modes with criticality and RPN analyses. |
| `fn_curve` | function | Return the F-N curve of ``(frequency, consequence)`` scenarios. |
| `fn_curve_from_event_trees` | function | Return the F-N curve of all sequences of one or more event trees. |
| `FNCriterion` | class | Criterion line ``F_c(N) = C (N_0 / N)^a``. |
| `FNCurve` | class | An F-N curve with an optional criterion comparison. |
| `FNPoint` | class | One step of an F-N curve and its comparison with a criterion. |
| `FragilityFit` | class | Maximum-likelihood lognormal fragility fit. |
| `Gate` | class | A logic gate combining child events. |
| `GATE_KINDS` | constant |  |
| `gev_return_level` | function | Return the GEV return level ``z_T`` exceeded with probability ``1/T``. |
| `gev_return_level_gradient` | function | Return ``grad z_T`` with respect to ``(mu, sigma, xi)``. |

## Cost analysis

Life-cycle cost, cost risk, learning curves, and maintenance economics.

### `probstat.cost` · experimental

Engineering cost analysis: life-cycle cost, cost risk, and maintenance economics.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `Activity` | class | One activity of an activity-on-node network. |
| `ActivityNetwork` | class | A validated acyclic activity-on-node network. |
| `ActivityRisk` | class | Simulated risk measures of one activity. |
| `ActivityTiming` | class | Early and late dates and floats of one activity. |
| `age_replacement_cost_rate` | function | Return ``C(T) = (c_p R(T) + c_f F(T)) / int_0^T R(t) dt``. |
| `AlternativeEvaluation` | class | Expected life-cycle and risk cost of one alternative. |
| `annuity_factor` | function | Return the present-worth factor of a uniform end-of-period series. |
| `capital_recovery_factor` | function | Return the factor converting a present amount to an equal annual series. |
| `compare_alternatives` | function | Rank alternatives by expected life-cycle cost plus risk cost. |
| `COMPOUNDING` | constant |  |
| `ConfidenceLevelRow` | class | Total cost at one confidence level by every method. |
| `continuous_rate` | function | Return the continuous rate equivalent to a discrete rate. |
| `CorrectiveCost` | class | Expected corrective-maintenance cost over a horizon. |
| `CORRELATION_KINDS` | constant |  |
| `COST_CATEGORIES` | constant |  |
| `cost_effectiveness_ratio` | function | Return ``cost / effectiveness`` (for example cost per unit availability). |
| `cost_for_joint_confidence` | function | Return the smallest sampled cost ``C`` with ``JCL(C, S) >= level``. |
| `cost_of_unreliability` | function | Return the expected (and discounted) cost of failures over ``[0, H]``. |
| `cost_risk_analysis` | function | Return the total-cost S-curve, confidence levels, and contingency. |
| `cost_schedule_frontier` | function | Return the iso-confidence frontier of (deadline, cost) pairs. |
| `cost_schedule_samples` | function | Load cost onto simulated durations and return paired samples. |
| `CostBreakdown` | class | Life-cycle cost totals of one realization of the element values. |
| `CostElement` | class | One cost-breakdown-structure element with a payment-time profile. |
| `CostRiskResult` | class | Total-cost S-curve by the method of moments and by Monte Carlo. |
| `critical_path` | function | Run the critical path method on fixed durations. |
| `CriticalPathResult` | class | Deterministic critical path method result. |
| `DeadlineProbability` | class | Probability of finishing by a deadline with an exact interval. |
| `DEFAULT_LEVELS` | constant |  |
| `DesignAlternative` | class | A design alternative for a reliability-cost trade-off. |
| `discount_factor` | function | Return the factor that converts an amount at ``time`` to time zero. |
| `discounted_rate_factor` | function | Return ``int_0^H exp(-r t) dt``: present value of a unit cost rate. |
| `equivalent_annual_cost` | function | Return the equivalent uniform annual cost of a present cost. |
| `escalate` | function | Return a base-year amount escalated to a later time. |
| `expected_backorders` | function | Return ``EBO(s) = E[(D - s)^+] = mu P(D >= s) - s P(D >= s + 1)``. |
| `expected_corrective_cost` | function | Return the expected number and cost of failures from an intensity. |
| `expected_failure_cost` | function | Return ``sum_i p_i C_i`` for mission failure modes. |

| `FAMILIES` | constant |  |
| `fit_total_distribution` | function | Return the lognormal or normal law with a given mean and deviation. |
| `FrontierPoint` | class | One point of the iso-confidence cost-schedule frontier. |
| `incremental_cost_effectiveness` | function | Return the incremental ratio ``(C_b - C_a) / (E_b - E_a)``. |
| `IsoConfidenceFrontier` | class | Cost-schedule pairs that all have the same joint confidence. |
| `joint_confidence_level` | function | Return the JCL ``P(cost <= C and schedule <= S)`` from joint samples. |
| `joint_cost_schedule_confidence` | function | Return the joint confidence of a (cost, deadline) pair. |
| `JointConfidenceLevel` | class | Joint cost-schedule confidence level from paired samples. |
| `learning_exponent` | function | Return ``b = log2(slope)`` for a learning-curve slope. |

| `LifetimeLaw` | class | Structural interface of a lifetime law used by the policies. |
| `LotApproximation` | class | Lot-midpoint approximation of a unit-theory lot cost. |
| `method_of_moments_total` | function | Return the exact mean and variance of ``c_0 + sum a_i X_i``. |
| `minimal_repair_overhaul` | function | Return the optimal overhaul period under minimal repair (power law). |
| `minimum_spares` | function | Return the smallest ``s`` with ``P(D <= s) >= target``. |

| `present_value` | function | Return the present value of one amount paid at ``time``. |

| `spare_demand_mean` | function | Return the Poisson demand mean ``mu = lambda n T``. |

| `glr_false_alarm_bound` | function | Return the union bound on a false GLR alarm within ``steps`` samples. |
| `glr_mean_shift` | function | Run the windowed GLR test for a step change of the mean. |
| `GlrResult` | class | Windowed GLR step-change test output. |
| `grade_requirement_probability` | function | Return the probability that the 3-sigma requirement holds over grades. |
| `GradeRequirementResult` | class | Probability that the 3-sigma pointing requirement is met over grades. |
| `GradeSobolResult` | class | Sobol indices of the knowledge error over the sensor grades. |
| `GrainGeometry` | class | Burning-surface geometry of a propellant grain as a function of web burned. |
| `GrowthAssessment` | class | Crow-AMSAA assessment of a test campaign. |
| `GuidanceSpec` | class | Vertical rise, pitch-over kick, then gravity turn. |
| `GyroStarTrackerModel` | class | Single-axis gyro plus star-tracker model (SI units). |

| `heat_shield_sizing` | function | Size an ablative heat shield from the convective heat load (engineering level). |

| `HeatShieldSizing` | class | Result of :func:`heat_shield_sizing`. |
| `heritage_prior` | function | Return a Beta prior on reliability from discounted heritage tests. |
| `ideal_rocket_performance` | function | Compute ideal-rocket performance with ``eta_c*`` and divergence losses. |
| `ideal_thrust_coefficient` | function | Return the ideal thrust coefficient ``C_F`` of a fixed-area-ratio nozzle. |
| `IdentifiabilityReport` | class | Posterior summary of the density/ballistic-coefficient split. |
| `ImpactEnvironment` | class | Binned impact angle and speed with a projectile density. |
| `InsertionWindow` | class | Target window for a successful insertion. |
| `instability_probability` | function | Estimate ``P(growth_rate > 0)`` by Monte Carlo, PCE and FORM. |
| `InstabilityProbabilityResult` | class | Three estimates of ``P(growth_rate > 0)`` and their comparison. |
| `isotropic_angle_bins` | function | Return equal-probability angle bins for an isotropic flux on a flat plate. |

| `knowledge_sobol_ranking` | function | Rank the sensor parameters by their Sobol indices on the knowledge error. |

| `koiter_imperfection_for_knockdown` | function | Return the imperfection ratio ``d = (1 - lambda)^2 / (c lambda)``. |
| `koiter_knockdown` | function | Solve ``(1 - lambda)^2 = c lambda d`` for the knockdown ``lambda``. |
| `koiter_knockdown_derivative` | function | Return ``d lambda / d d`` of the Koiter law (implicit differentiation). |
| `launch_event_tree` | function | Build the launch event tree of the vehicle. |
| `LaunchVehicleDesign` | class | A two-stage launch vehicle described by its failure probabilities. |

| `lognormal_from_mean_cov` | function | Return the lognormal law with the given mean and coefficient of variation. |
| `maximum_compliant_altitude` | function | Return the highest initial altitude with ``P(non-compliance) <= target``. |

| `mean_pnp_quadrature` | function | Return ``E[PNP]`` and its Jensen gap by tensor Gauss-Hermite quadrature. |
| `minimum_tank_pressure_for_probability` | function | Find the smallest mean tank pressure with ``P(margin < 1) = target``. |
| `MixtureRatioTable` | class | User-supplied table ``O/F -> (T_c, gamma, M)`` with interpolation. |
| `MixtureState` | class | Chamber gas state at one mixture ratio. |

| `mmod_risk_model` | function | Build a :class:`~probstat.modeling.ProbabilisticModel` of MMOD risk. |
| `mode_shape` | function | Return the complex pressure ``p`` and scaled velocity ``w = rho0 c u``. |
| `monte_carlo_collision_probability` | function | Estimate ``Pc`` by sampling the three-dimensional relative position. |

| `nhpp_mission_reliability` | function | Return the probability of no failure over one mission under the fit. |

## Method development

Estimator combinators, certification checks, pre-specified simulation studies, and candidate selection.

### `probstat.methodlab` · experimental

A laboratory for deriving new estimators and certifying their claims.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `antithetic_estimator` | function | Derive the antithetic-pair estimator of an integral over the unit cube. |
| `best_linear_unbiased_weights` | function | Return the minimum-variance weights for unbiased estimators of one mean. |
| `bootstrap_bias_corrected` | function | Derive the bootstrap bias-corrected version of a statistic. |
| `check_convergence_rate` | function | Test a claimed decay rate of the mean squared error. |
| `check_coverage` | function | Test the claim that an interval procedure covers at its nominal level. |
| `check_efficiency_gain` | function | Test the claim that a candidate is more efficient than a baseline. |
| `check_invariance` | function | Check a metamorphic relation of a deterministic procedure. |
| `check_unbiasedness` | function | Test the claim that an estimator is unbiased for a known value. |
| `check_variance` | function | Test a claimed variance of an estimator. |
| `check_variance_stability` | function | Look for symptoms of an infinite variance. |
| `CheckResult` | class | The outcome of one certification check. |
| `combine_unbiased` | function | Derive the best linear unbiased combination from a pilot run. |
| `compare_sequentially` | function | Compare two methods on paired problems with optional stopping. |
| `control_variate_estimator` | function | Derive a control-variate estimator with pilot-estimated coefficients. |
| `coupled_sum_estimator` | function | Derive the sum-type randomized-truncation estimator (independent sum). |
| `DataGeneratingMechanism` | class | A named way of simulating one data set with a known estimand value. |
| `Draw` | class | One replicate of an estimator and the work it cost. |
| `EfficiencyRanking` | class | Candidate estimators ordered by work-normalized variance. |
| `empirical_influence` | function | Differentiate a weighted functional to get influence values. |
| `Estimator` | class | A named, seeded estimator with the record of how it was derived. |
| `estimator_from_function` | function | Wrap a function of a random generator as a base estimator. |
| `extrapolated_estimator` | function | Derive the Richardson-extrapolated combination of a family. |
| `extrapolation_weights` | function | Return weights that cancel the leading terms of a bias expansion. |
| `finite_levels` | function | Return a level law with the given finite probability vector. |
| `geometric_levels` | function | Return the geometric level law ``P(N = n) = (1 - ratio) ratio^n``. |
| `HalvingResult` | class | Outcome of sequential halving. |
| `HalvingRound` | class | One round of sequential halving. |
| `InfluenceAnalysis` | class | Empirical influence values of a functional and what follows from them. |
| `interval_verdict` | function | Return the verdict implied by a confidence interval. |
| `InvarianceResult` | class | Outcome of a metamorphic relation over generated cases. |
| `jackknife_bias_corrected` | function | Derive the jackknife bias-corrected version of a statistic. |
| `LevelDistribution` | class | A distribution of the random truncation level ``N`` on ``0, 1, 2, ...``. |
| `LinearCombination` | class | Weights of a linear combination and the variance they predict. |
| `MEASURES` | constant |  |
| `Method` | class | A named analysis method applied to one data set. |
| `method_card` | function | Assemble the method card of an estimator. |
| `MethodCard` | class | The claims of one method and the evidence gathered for each. |
| `MethodOutput` | class | What one method returns for one data set. |
| `one_step_estimator` | function | Correct a plug-in estimate by the mean of its influence function. |
| `OneStepEstimate` | class | A plug-in estimate corrected by the mean of an influence function. |
| `optimal_level_probabilities` | function | Return the level probabilities that minimize second moment times cost. |
| `Performance` | class | Performance measures of one method under one mechanism. |
| `performance_measures` | function | Compute performance measures with their Monte Carlo standard errors. |
| `pilot_covariance` | function | Estimate the covariance matrix of jointly drawn estimators. |
| `PrecisionComparison` | class | Relative precision of one method against a reference, paired. |
| `rank_by_efficiency` | function | Rank candidate estimators by work-normalized variance. |
| `repetitions_for_standard_error` | function | Return the repetitions needed for a target Monte Carlo standard error. |
| `ReplicateSummary` | class | Sample moments of a set of replicates of one estimator. |
| `run_simulation_study` | function | Execute a pre-specified simulation study. |
| `sample_mean` | function | Derive the mean of ``size`` independent draws of an estimator. |
| `sequential_halving` | function | Select the best of several candidates with a fixed evaluation budget. |
| `SequentialComparison` | class | Outcome of an anytime-valid paired comparison of two methods. |
| `SimulationStudyResult` | class | Performance of every method under every mechanism. |
| `single_term_estimator` | function | Derive the single-term randomized-truncation estimator. |
| `StudyProtocol` | class | The pre-specified design of a simulation study (ADEMP). |
| `sum_of_estimators` | function | Derive the sum of independently drawn estimators. |
| `summarize` | function | Replicate an estimator and summarize the draws. |
| `summarize_draws` | function | Summarize replicate draws by their sample moments. |
| `thinned_estimator` | function | Derive an estimator that is evaluated only with a given probability. |
| `truncation_design` | function | Evaluate a level law for the single-term estimator on finite levels. |
| `TruncationDesign` | class | Second moment and expected cost of a single-term estimator. |
| `VarianceStability` | class | Evidence on whether the variance of an estimator is finite. |
| `VERDICTS` | constant |  |

## Orchestration and figures

The `UQStudy` workflow and the themed figure library.

### `probstat.workflow` · provisional

Integrated studies: UQ on one probabilistic model, and mission assessment.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `DesignPointAnalysis` | class | Several design points of one failure event and their system estimates. |
| `DesignRecommendation` | class | Greedy Bayesian design of new experiments for the study's calibration. |
| `DiscrepancyAnalysis` | class | Kennedy-O'Hagan calibration of one QoI with its diagnostics. |
| `DiscrepancyStudyPrediction` | class | Simulator-only and bias-corrected predictions after a KOH calibration. |
| `EmpiricalMarginal` | class | Continuous law whose quantile function is the type-7 sample quantile. |
| `InformationSensitivity` | class | Mutual information between each input and one QoI. |
| `input_law_hash` | function | Return a stable 12-hex-digit hash of a joint input law. |
| `InverseStageRecord` | class | One stage run of an :class:`InverseStudy`, with cost and provenance. |
| `InverseStudy` | class | Run the inverse-problem stages on one problem and write one report. |
| `MissionAssessment` | class | Probabilistic mission assessment over one exact system model. |
| `MissionAssessmentResult` | class | Everything one mission assessment produced. |
| `MissionCost` | class | Life-cycle cost plus the expected cost of mission losses. |
| `MissionReliability` | class | Exact mission-level reliability measures. |
| `MissionUncertainty` | class | Epistemic distribution of the mission reliability. |
| `posterior_joint_input` | function | Approximate a posterior by a marginals-plus-Gaussian-copula input law. |
| `POSTERIOR_METHODS` | constant |  |
| `render_mission_report` | function | Render a :class:`MissionAssessmentResult` as Markdown. |
| `ReportTable` | class | A small pre-formatted table of one report section. |
| `ResolutionResult` | class | Matrix-free resolution probes and LIS rank at one linearization point. |
| `SampleSummary` | class | Posterior samples with their Monte Carlo diagnostics. |
| `SpectrumResult` | class | Spectrum of the prior-whitened Jacobian ``K = W J P``. |
| `STAGE_STATUSES` | constant |  |
| `StageRecord` | class | One analysis stage of a study, with its cost and provenance. |
| `StudyComparison` | class | Sample moments compared with a reference. |
| `TailAnalysis` | class | Peaks-over-threshold analysis of one QoI tail, in QoI units. |
| `TargetedPropagation` | class | Monte Carlo propagation of one QoI sized by a relative-error target. |
| `UQStudy` | class | Run propagation, sensitivity, reliability, and risk on one model. |

### `probstat.visualization` · provisional

Optional explanatory plots built on stable domain APIs.

| Name | Kind | Summary |
|---|---|---|
| `active_palette` | function | Return the palette of the innermost active theme context. |
| `chart_theme` | function | Activate the chart theme for the enclosed plotting code. |
| `ChartPalette` | class | Colour tokens for one chart theme. |
| `GRAPHITE` | constant |  |
| `plot_bernoulli_pmf` | function | Plot and annotate the two masses of a Bernoulli distribution. |
| `plot_categorical_pmf` | function | Plot an unordered categorical PMF without imposing numeric meaning. |
| `plot_characteristic_function` | function | Plot real and imaginary parts of a finite characteristic function. |
| `plot_cobweb` | function | Draw a cobweb (parallel-coordinate) plot with exceeding runs highlighted. |
| `plot_continuous_functions` | function | Plot the density above, and CDF with survival below, on a shared x-axis. |
| `plot_efficiency_comparison` | function | Plot cost-normalized efficiency gains against a common baseline. |
| `plot_empirical_cdf` | function | Plot the right-continuous empirical CDF of a finite sample. |
| `plot_finite_measure` | function | Plot outcome masses and optionally highlight one finite event. |
| `plot_fitted_cdf` | function | Compare an empirical CDF with a fitted or specified model CDF. |
| `plot_input_scatter` | function | Draw the output against each input as a grid of scatterplots. |
| `plot_integer_pmf` | function | Plot an integer-valued PMF over an explicit finite viewing window. |
| `plot_joint_pmf` | function | Plot a finite joint PMF as an annotated single-hue mass matrix. |
| `plot_monte_carlo_convergence` | function | Plot nested Monte Carlo estimates and their reported confidence band. |
| `plot_normal_pdf` | function | Plot a normal density and shade the central one-sigma mass. |
| `plot_random_variable_pmf` | function | Plot the induced PMF of a finite random variable. |
| `plot_refinement_convergence` | function | Visualize three-level convergence against Richardson extrapolation. |
| `plot_tornado` | function | Draw one-at-a-time swings as horizontal bars around the baseline output. |
| `plot_waiting_count_pmfs` | function | Compare two failures-before-success PMFs on one integer window. |
| `PORCELAIN` | constant |  |
| `save_figure` | function | Save a figure with the theme canvas, tight bounds, and padding. |
